# 파일별 전체 소스 코드

실제 구현 파일의 UTF-8 원문입니다. DB 비밀번호는 포함하지 않습니다.

## pom.xml

```xml
<?xml version="1.0" ?>
<project xmlns="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0" xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance" xsi:schemaLocation="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0 https://maven.apache.org/xsd/maven-4.0.0.xsd">
  <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>
  <groupId>kr.ac.yumin</groupId>
  <artifactId>JavaTermProject</artifactId>
  <version>1.0.0</version>
  <properties>
    <maven.compiler.release>17</maven.compiler.release>
    <project.build.sourceEncoding>UTF-8</project.build.sourceEncoding>
  </properties>
  <dependencies>
    <dependency><groupId>org.jsoup</groupId><artifactId>jsoup</artifactId><version>1.21.2</version></dependency>
    <dependency><groupId>com.formdev</groupId><artifactId>flatlaf</artifactId><version>3.8</version></dependency>
    <dependency><groupId>com.googlecode.soundlibs</groupId><artifactId>mp3spi</artifactId><version>1.9.5.4</version></dependency>
    <dependency>
      <groupId>com.oracle.database.jdbc</groupId>
      <artifactId>ojdbc11</artifactId>
      <version>21.18.0.0</version>
    </dependency>
    <dependency>
      <groupId>org.junit.jupiter</groupId>
      <artifactId>junit-jupiter</artifactId>
      <version>5.11.4</version>
      <scope>test</scope>
    </dependency>
  </dependencies>
  <build>
    <plugins>
      <plugin>
        <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
        <artifactId>maven-compiler-plugin</artifactId>
        <version>3.13.0</version>
      </plugin>
      <plugin>
        <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
        <artifactId>maven-surefire-plugin</artifactId>
        <version>3.5.2</version>
        <configuration>
          <excludedGroups>oracle</excludedGroups>
        </configuration>
      </plugin>
      <plugin>
        <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
        <artifactId>maven-shade-plugin</artifactId>
        <version>3.6.0</version>
        <executions>
          <execution>
            <phase>package</phase>
            <goals>
              <goal>shade</goal>
            </goals>
            <configuration>
              <createDependencyReducedPom>false</createDependencyReducedPom>
              <filters>
                <filter>
                  <artifact>*:*</artifact>
                  <excludes>
                    <exclude>META-INF/*.SF</exclude>
                    <exclude>META-INF/*.RSA</exclude>
                    <exclude>META-INF/*.DSA</exclude>
                  </excludes>
                </filter>
              </filters>
              <transformers>
                <transformer implementation="org.apache.maven.plugins.shade.resource.ManifestResourceTransformer">
                  <mainClass>kr.ac.yumin.playlist.App</mainClass>
                </transformer>
                <transformer implementation="org.apache.maven.plugins.shade.resource.ServicesResourceTransformer"/>
              </transformers>
            </configuration>
          </execution>
        </executions>
      </plugin>
    </plugins>
  </build>
  <profiles>
    <profile>
      <id>oracle-test</id>
      <build>
        <plugins>
          <plugin>
            <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
            <artifactId>maven-surefire-plugin</artifactId>
            <configuration>
              <excludedGroups>none</excludedGroups>
            </configuration>
          </plugin>
        </plugins>
      </build>
    </profile>
  </profiles>
</project>
```

## sql/02_add_sequence.sql

```sql
-- 기존 데이터 보존. 프로그램을 모두 종료한 상태에서 최초 한 번 실행합니다.
-- 기존 시퀀스가 있으면 변경하지 않습니다. MAX는 최초 초기화에만 사용합니다.
SET SERVEROUTPUT ON;
DECLARE
  v_exists NUMBER;
  v_start NUMBER;
BEGIN
  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_exists FROM user_sequences WHERE sequence_name='SEQ_PLAYLIST';
  IF v_exists=0 THEN
    SELECT GREATEST(NVL(MAX(playlist_id),0)+1,1) INTO v_start FROM PLAYLIST;
    EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'CREATE SEQUENCE SEQ_PLAYLIST START WITH ' || TO_CHAR(v_start,'FM99999999990') || ' INCREMENT BY 1 MAXVALUE 9999999999 NOCYCLE NOCACHE';
    DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('SEQ_PLAYLIST 생성 완료');
  ELSE
    DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('기존 SEQ_PLAYLIST 유지');
  END IF;
END;
/
```

## sql/06_music_and_membership.sql

```sql
-- 기존 7개 테이블과 예시 데이터는 보존한다. SQL Developer에서 F5로 실행.
SET DEFINE OFF;
DECLARE
  n NUMBER;
BEGIN
  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO n FROM user_tables WHERE table_name='SONG_MEDIA';
  IF n=0 THEN EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'CREATE TABLE SONG_MEDIA (
    song_id NUMBER(10) CONSTRAINT pk_song_media PRIMARY KEY,
    resource_path VARCHAR2(255 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    performer VARCHAR2(120 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    source_url VARCHAR2(1000 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    license_name VARCHAR2(120 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_media_song FOREIGN KEY(song_id) REFERENCES SONG(song_id))'; END IF;
  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO n FROM user_tables WHERE table_name='MEMBERSHIP_ORDER';
  IF n=0 THEN EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'CREATE TABLE MEMBERSHIP_ORDER (
    order_id VARCHAR2(36 CHAR) CONSTRAINT pk_membership_order PRIMARY KEY,
    member_id VARCHAR2(30 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    amount_won NUMBER(8) NOT NULL,
    paid_at TIMESTAMP(6) DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP NOT NULL,
    valid_until TIMESTAMP(6) NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR2(10 CHAR) DEFAULT ''PAID'' NOT NULL,
    payment_method VARCHAR2(10 CHAR) DEFAULT ''DEMO'' NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_order_member FOREIGN KEY(member_id) REFERENCES MEMBER(member_id),
    CONSTRAINT ck_order_status CHECK(status IN (''PAID'',''CANCELLED'')),
    CONSTRAINT ck_order_demo CHECK(payment_method=''DEMO''),
    CONSTRAINT ck_order_amount CHECK(amount_won=4900))'; END IF;
END;
/
```

## sql/08_melon_chart.sql

```sql
-- Melon TOP100 metadata extension. Original rows and keys are preserved.
SET DEFINE OFF;
DECLARE
  n NUMBER;
BEGIN
  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO n FROM user_tab_columns WHERE table_name='SONG' AND column_name='DURATION_SECONDS' AND nullable='N';
  IF n=1 THEN EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'ALTER TABLE SONG MODIFY(duration_seconds NULL)'; END IF;
  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO n FROM user_tables WHERE table_name='MELON_CHART';
  IF n=0 THEN EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'CREATE TABLE MELON_CHART (
    chart_at TIMESTAMP(0) CONSTRAINT pk_melon_chart PRIMARY KEY,
    fetched_at TIMESTAMP(6) DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP NOT NULL,
    source_url VARCHAR2(500 CHAR) NOT NULL,
    row_count NUMBER(3) NOT NULL CHECK(row_count=100))'; END IF;
  SELECT COUNT(*) INTO n FROM user_tables WHERE table_name='MELON_CHART_ENTRY';
  IF n=0 THEN EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'CREATE TABLE MELON_CHART_ENTRY (
    chart_at TIMESTAMP(0) NOT NULL,
    chart_rank NUMBER(3) NOT NULL CHECK(chart_rank BETWEEN 1 AND 100),
    song_id NUMBER(10) NOT NULL,
    melon_song_id NUMBER(10) NOT NULL,
    cover_url VARCHAR2(1000 CHAR),
    CONSTRAINT pk_melon_chart_entry PRIMARY KEY(chart_at,chart_rank),
    CONSTRAINT fk_chart_entry_chart FOREIGN KEY(chart_at) REFERENCES MELON_CHART(chart_at),
    CONSTRAINT fk_chart_entry_song FOREIGN KEY(song_id) REFERENCES SONG(song_id))'; END IF;
END;
/
COMMIT;
```

## run.bat

```text
@echo off
chcp 65001 >nul
cd /d "%~dp0"
if not exist target\JavaTermProject-1.0.0.jar (
  echo IntelliJ Maven에서 package를 먼저 실행해 주세요.
  pause
  exit /b 1
)
if defined JAVA_HOME (
  "%JAVA_HOME%\bin\javaw.exe" -jar target\JavaTermProject-1.0.0.jar
) else if exist "%USERPROFILE%\.jdks\jdk-17.0.18+8\bin\javaw.exe" (
  "%USERPROFILE%\.jdks\jdk-17.0.18+8\bin\javaw.exe" -jar target\JavaTermProject-1.0.0.jar
) else (
  javaw -jar target\JavaTermProject-1.0.0.jar
)
```

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.ui.*;

import javax.swing.SwingUtilities;

public final class App {
    private App() {}

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        SwingUtilities.invokeLater(
                () -> {
                    Ui.theme();
                    new MainFrame().setVisible(true);
                });
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/audio/AudioPlayer.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.audio;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.*;

import java.io.*;
import java.util.concurrent.*;

import javax.sound.sampled.*;

/** 실제 MP3를 PCM으로 디코딩하여 사운드 장치로 출력한다. 제한은 UI 타이머가 아닌 출력 프레임에서 적용한다. */
public final class AudioPlayer implements AutoCloseable {
    private final ExecutorService worker =
            Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(
                    r -> {
                        Thread t = new Thread(r, "muse-audio");
                        t.setDaemon(true);
                        return t;
                    });
    private volatile long generation;
    private volatile boolean paused, playing, limited;
    private volatile double position, seek = -1;
    private volatile float volume = .65f;
    private volatile String error;
    private volatile SourceDataLine line;
    private volatile PlaybackPolicy policy;
    private volatile MediaTrack track;

    public synchronized void play(MediaTrack next, PassStatus pass) {
        stop();
        track = next;
        policy = new PlaybackPolicy(pass);
        position = 0;
        seek = 0;
        error = null;
        limited = false;
        paused = false;
        playing = true;
        long ticket = generation;
        worker.submit(() -> decode(ticket, next));
    }

    public void updatePass(PassStatus pass) {
        PlaybackPolicy current = policy;
        if (current != null) current.update(pass);
    }

    public void togglePause() {
        if (playing) paused = !paused;
    }

    public void seek(double seconds) {
        PlaybackPolicy p = policy;
        MediaTrack t = track;
        if (p != null && t != null && playing) {
            seek = p.clampSeek(seconds, t.song().seconds());
            limited = false;
        }
    }

    public void volume(float value) {
        volume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, value));
    }

    public double position() {
        return position;
    }

    public boolean playing() {
        return playing;
    }

    public boolean paused() {
        return paused;
    }

    public boolean limited() {
        return limited;
    }

    public String error() {
        return error;
    }

    public MediaTrack track() {
        return track;
    }

    public int limit() {
        return track == null || policy == null ? 60 : policy.limit(track.song().seconds());
    }

    public synchronized void stop() {
        generation++;
        playing = false;
        paused = false;
        SourceDataLine out = line;
        if (out != null) {
            out.stop();
            out.flush();
            out.close();
        }
        line = null;
    }

    private AudioInputStream pcm(MediaTrack t) throws Exception {
        InputStream resource;
        if (t.resource().matches("local-audio/[a-f0-9-]+[.]mp3")) {
            resource =
                    java.nio.file.Files.newInputStream(
                            java.nio.file.Path.of(
                                    "config/audio",
                                    t.resource().substring("local-audio/".length())));
        } else {
            resource = AudioPlayer.class.getClassLoader().getResourceAsStream(t.resource());
        }
        if (resource == null) throw new FileNotFoundException("음원 파일을 찾을 수 없습니다.");
        AudioInputStream source;
        try {
            source = AudioSystem.getAudioInputStream(new BufferedInputStream(resource));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            resource.close();
            throw e;
        }
        AudioFormat f = source.getFormat();
        AudioFormat decoded =
                new AudioFormat(
                        AudioFormat.Encoding.PCM_SIGNED,
                        f.getSampleRate(),
                        16,
                        f.getChannels(),
                        f.getChannels() * 2,
                        f.getSampleRate(),
                        false);
        try {
            return AudioSystem.getAudioInputStream(decoded, source);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            source.close();
            throw e;
        }
    }

    private void decode(long ticket, MediaTrack selected) {
        try {
            while (ticket == generation) {
                double start = seek >= 0 ? seek : position;
                seek = -1;
                try (AudioInputStream input = pcm(selected)) {
                    AudioFormat format = input.getFormat();
                    int frameSize = format.getFrameSize();
                    double rate = format.getSampleRate();
                    long base = (long) (start * rate), skip = base * frameSize;
                    byte[] buffer = new byte[4096 - (4096 % frameSize)];
                    while (skip > 0 && ticket == generation) {
                        int n = input.read(buffer, 0, (int) Math.min(buffer.length, skip));
                        if (n < 0) {
                            skip = 0;
                            break;
                        }
                        skip -= n;
                    }
                    if (ticket != generation) return;
                    SourceDataLine output = AudioSystem.getSourceDataLine(format);
                    output.open(format, Math.max(buffer.length, frameSize * (int) (rate / 8)));
                    line = output;
                    output.start();
                    long written = base;
                    boolean restart = false;
                    try {
                        while (ticket == generation) {
                            position = (base + output.getLongFramePosition()) / rate;
                            if (seek >= 0) {
                                restart = true;
                                output.flush();
                                break;
                            }
                            if (paused) {
                                output.stop();
                                Thread.sleep(25);
                                continue;
                            } else if (!output.isRunning()) output.start();
                            long ceiling = (long) (policy.limit(selected.song().seconds()) * rate);
                            if (written >= ceiling) {
                                output.drain();
                                position = Math.min(written, ceiling) / rate;
                                limited =
                                        policy.limit(selected.song().seconds())
                                                < selected.song().seconds();
                                break;
                            }
                            int wanted =
                                    (int) Math.min(buffer.length, (ceiling - written) * frameSize);
                            int n = input.read(buffer, 0, wanted);
                            if (n < 0) {
                                output.drain();
                                position = written / rate;
                                break;
                            }
                            scale(buffer, n, volume);
                            int offset = 0;
                            while (offset < n && ticket == generation) {
                                int count = output.write(buffer, offset, n - offset);
                                if (count <= 0) break;
                                offset += count;
                            }
                            written += offset / frameSize;
                        }
                    } finally {
                        output.stop();
                        output.close();
                        if (line == output) line = null;
                    }
                    if (!restart) break;
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception failure) {
            if (ticket == generation) error = "음원을 재생할 수 없습니다. 기본 오디오 출력 장치와 음원 파일을 확인해 주세요.";
        } finally {
            if (ticket == generation) playing = false;
        }
    }

    private static void scale(byte[] bytes, int length, float volume) {
        for (int i = 0; i + 1 < length; i += 2) {
            short original = (short) ((bytes[i] & 255) | (bytes[i + 1] << 8));
            short value = (short) (original * volume);
            bytes[i] = (byte) value;
            bytes[i + 1] = (byte) (value >> 8);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        stop();
        worker.shutdownNow();
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/audio/PlaybackPolicy.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.audio;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.PassStatus;

import java.time.Duration;

/** DB 시각과 만료일의 차이를 단조 시계로 바꾸므로 재생 중 만료도 반영한다. */
public final class PlaybackPolicy {
    public static final int FREE_SECONDS = 60;
    private volatile long premiumUntilNanos;

    public PlaybackPolicy(PassStatus status) {
        update(status);
    }

    public void update(PassStatus status) {
        long remaining =
                status.active()
                        ? Duration.between(status.databaseNow(), status.expiresAt()).toNanos()
                        : 0;
        premiumUntilNanos = System.nanoTime() + remaining;
    }

    public int limit(int duration) {
        return System.nanoTime() < premiumUntilNanos ? duration : Math.min(FREE_SECONDS, duration);
    }

    public double clampSeek(double seconds, int duration) {
        return Math.max(0, Math.min(seconds, limit(duration)));
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/config/Database.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.config;

import java.io.*;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.*;
import java.sql.*;
import java.util.Properties;

/** 접속 정보는 실행 폴더의 외부 파일에서만 읽는다. */
public final class Database {
    private Database() {}

    public static Path settingsPath() {
        return Path.of(System.getProperty("playlist.config", "config/db.properties"));
    }

    public static Properties settings() {
        Properties p = new Properties();
        try (Reader in = Files.newBufferedReader(settingsPath(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
            p.load(in);
        } catch (IOException ignored) {
        }
        return p;
    }

    public static void testAndSave(String url, String username, char[] password)
            throws SQLException, IOException {
        Properties p = new Properties();
        p.setProperty("db.url", url.strip());
        p.setProperty("db.username", username.strip());
        p.setProperty("db.password", new String(password));
        java.util.Arrays.fill(password, '\0');
        try (Connection c = connect(p)) {
            try (PreparedStatement statement = c.prepareStatement("SELECT 1 FROM DUAL");
                    ResultSet result = statement.executeQuery()) {
                if (!result.next()) throw new SQLException("DB 연결을 확인할 수 없습니다.");
            }
        }
        Path path = settingsPath().toAbsolutePath();
        Files.createDirectories(path.getParent());
        try (Writer out = Files.newBufferedWriter(path, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
            p.store(out, "Private Oracle connection settings. Never upload.");
        }
    }

    private static Connection connect(Properties p) throws SQLException {
        for (String key : new String[] {"db.url", "db.username", "db.password"})
            if (p.getProperty(key, "").isBlank())
                throw new SQLException("DB 설정의 URL, 사용자 이름, 비밀번호를 모두 입력해 주세요.");
        Properties info = new Properties();
        info.setProperty("user", p.getProperty("db.username"));
        info.setProperty("password", p.getProperty("db.password"));
        info.setProperty("oracle.net.CONNECT_TIMEOUT", "7000");
        info.setProperty("oracle.jdbc.ReadTimeout", "15000");
        return DriverManager.getConnection(p.getProperty("db.url"), info);
    }

    public static Connection open() throws SQLException {
        if (!Files.exists(settingsPath()))
            throw new SQLException("config/db.properties를 만들고 Oracle 접속 정보를 입력해 주세요.");
        return connect(settings());
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/dao/Jdbc.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.dao;

import java.sql.*;
import java.util.*;

/** 한 연결에서 실행하며 모든 JDBC 자원을 try-with-resources로 닫는다. */
public final class Jdbc {
    private Jdbc() {}

    @FunctionalInterface
    public interface Mapper<T> {
        T read(ResultSet r) throws SQLException;
    }

    public static PreparedStatement prepare(Connection c, String sql, Object... args)
            throws SQLException {
        PreparedStatement p = c.prepareStatement(sql);
        try {
            p.setQueryTimeout(20);
            for (int i = 0; i < args.length; i++) p.setObject(i + 1, args[i]);
            return p;
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            p.close();
            throw e;
        }
    }

    public static int update(Connection c, String sql, Object... args) throws SQLException {
        try (PreparedStatement p = prepare(c, sql, args)) {
            return p.executeUpdate();
        }
    }

    public static <T> List<T> list(Connection c, String sql, Mapper<T> mapper, Object... args)
            throws SQLException {
        try (PreparedStatement p = prepare(c, sql, args);
                ResultSet r = p.executeQuery()) {
            List<T> out = new ArrayList<>();
            while (r.next()) out.add(mapper.read(r));
            return out;
        }
    }

    public static long number(Connection c, String sql, Object... args) throws SQLException {
        return list(c, sql, r -> r.getLong(1), args).get(0);
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/dao/MediaDao.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.dao;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.*;

import java.sql.*;
import java.util.*;

public final class MediaDao {
    private static final String COLUMNS =
            """
SELECT s.song_id,s.title,
(SELECT LISTAGG(a.artist_name,', ') WITHIN GROUP(ORDER BY a.artist_id)
 FROM SONG_ARTIST sa JOIN ARTIST a ON a.artist_id=sa.artist_id WHERE sa.song_id=s.song_id),
s.album_title,s.genre,s.duration_seconds,m.resource_path,m.performer,m.source_url,m.license_name
""";

    public List<MediaTrack> catalog(Connection c) throws SQLException {
        return Jdbc.list(
                c,
                COLUMNS
                        + """
                          ,e.chart_rank,e.cover_url,TO_CHAR(e.chart_at,'YYYY.MM.DD HH24:MI')
                          FROM MELON_CHART_ENTRY e JOIN SONG s ON s.song_id=e.song_id
                          LEFT JOIN SONG_MEDIA m ON m.song_id=s.song_id
                          WHERE e.chart_at=(SELECT MAX(chart_at) FROM MELON_CHART)
                          ORDER BY e.chart_rank
                          """,
                r ->
                        new MediaTrack(
                                song(r),
                                r.getString(7),
                                r.getString(8),
                                r.getString(9),
                                r.getString(10),
                                r.getInt(11),
                                r.getString(12),
                                r.getString(13)));
    }

    public MediaTrack find(Connection c, long id) throws SQLException {
        return Jdbc.list(
                        c,
                        COLUMNS
                                + " FROM SONG s LEFT JOIN SONG_MEDIA m ON m.song_id=s.song_id WHERE"
                                + " s.song_id=?",
                        r ->
                                new MediaTrack(
                                        song(r),
                                        r.getString(7),
                                        r.getString(8),
                                        r.getString(9),
                                        r.getString(10)),
                        id)
                .stream()
                .findFirst()
                .orElse(null);
    }

    private Song song(ResultSet r) throws SQLException {
        return new Song(
                r.getLong(1),
                r.getString(2),
                r.getString(3),
                r.getString(4),
                r.getString(5),
                r.getInt(6),
                0);
    }
}
```

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.dao;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.Member;

public final class MemberDao {

                        c,
                        "SELECT member_id,nickname,email,password_hash FROM MEMBER WHERE"
                                + " member_id=?",
                        r ->
                                new Credentials(
                                        new Member(r.getString(1), r.getString(2), r.getString(3)),
                                        r.getString(4)),
                        id)
                .stream()
                .findFirst();

        Jdbc.update(
                c,
                "INSERT INTO MEMBER(member_id,password_hash,nickname,email) VALUES(?,?,?,?)",
                m.id(),
                hash,
                m.nickname(),
                m.email());
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/dao/PlaylistDao.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.dao;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.*;

import java.sql.*;
import java.util.*;

public final class PlaylistDao {
    private static final String SELECT =
            """
SELECT p.playlist_id,p.title,p.description,p.is_public,p.member_id,m.nickname,p.created_at,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM PLAYLIST_LIKE l WHERE l.playlist_id=p.playlist_id) likes,
  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM PLAYLIST_LIKE l WHERE l.playlist_id=p.playlist_id AND l.member_id=?) liked
FROM PLAYLIST p JOIN MEMBER m ON m.member_id=p.member_id
""";

    private Playlist map(ResultSet r) throws SQLException {
        return new Playlist(
                r.getLong(1),
                r.getString(2),
                r.getString(3),
                "Y".equals(r.getString(4)),
                r.getString(5),
                r.getString(6),
                r.getTimestamp(7).toLocalDateTime(),
                r.getInt(8),
                r.getInt(9) > 0);
    }

    public List<Playlist> mine(Connection c, String user) throws SQLException {
        return Jdbc.list(
                c,
                SELECT + " WHERE p.member_id=? ORDER BY p.created_at DESC,p.playlist_id DESC",
                this::map,
                user,
                user);
    }

    public List<Playlist> publicLists(Connection c, String user, String query) throws SQLException {
        return Jdbc.list(
                c,
                SELECT
                        + " WHERE p.is_public='Y' AND INSTR(LOWER(p.title),LOWER(?))>0 ORDER BY"
                        + " p.created_at DESC,p.playlist_id DESC",
                this::map,
                user,
                query);
    }

    public List<Playlist> allPublic(Connection c, String user) throws SQLException {
        return Jdbc.list(
                c,
                SELECT + " WHERE p.is_public='Y' ORDER BY p.created_at DESC,p.playlist_id DESC",
                this::map,
                user);
    }

    public Optional<Playlist> visible(Connection c, String user, long id) throws SQLException {
        return Jdbc.list(
                        c,
                        SELECT + " WHERE p.playlist_id=? AND (p.member_id=? OR p.is_public='Y')",
                        this::map,
                        user,
                        id,
                        user)
                .stream()
                .findFirst();
    }

    /** 잠금 후 소유자를 다시 검사한다. 모든 쓰기 작업이 같은 부모 행을 잠근다. */
    public boolean lockOwner(Connection c, String user, long id) throws SQLException {
        return !Jdbc.list(
                        c,
                        "SELECT playlist_id FROM PLAYLIST WHERE playlist_id=? AND member_id=? FOR"
                                + " UPDATE WAIT 5",
                        r -> r.getLong(1),
                        id,
                        user)
                .isEmpty();
    }

    public boolean lockPublic(Connection c, long id) throws SQLException {
        return !Jdbc.list(
                        c,
                        "SELECT playlist_id FROM PLAYLIST WHERE playlist_id=? AND is_public='Y' FOR"
                                + " UPDATE WAIT 5",
                        r -> r.getLong(1),
                        id)
                .isEmpty();
    }

    public long create(Connection c, String user, String title, String description, boolean pub)
            throws SQLException {
        long id = Jdbc.number(c, "SELECT SEQ_PLAYLIST.NEXTVAL FROM DUAL");
        Jdbc.update(
                c,
                "INSERT INTO PLAYLIST(playlist_id,title,description,is_public,member_id)"
                        + " VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)",
                id,
                title,
                description,
                pub ? "Y" : "N",
                user);
        return id;
    }

    public int edit(Connection c, String user, long id, String title, String desc, boolean pub)
            throws SQLException {
        return Jdbc.update(
                c,
                "UPDATE PLAYLIST SET title=?,description=?,is_public=? WHERE playlist_id=? AND"
                        + " member_id=?",
                title,
                desc,
                pub ? "Y" : "N",
                id,
                user);
    }

    public int delete(Connection c, String user, long id) throws SQLException {
        return Jdbc.update(c, "DELETE FROM PLAYLIST WHERE playlist_id=? AND member_id=?", id, user);
    }

    public void like(Connection c, String user, long id, boolean value) throws SQLException {
        if (value)
            Jdbc.update(
                    c,
                    """
INSERT INTO PLAYLIST_LIKE(member_id,playlist_id)
SELECT ?,playlist_id FROM PLAYLIST p WHERE p.playlist_id=? AND p.is_public='Y'
AND NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM PLAYLIST_LIKE l WHERE l.member_id=? AND l.playlist_id=p.playlist_id)
""",
                    user,
                    id,
                    user);
        else
            Jdbc.update(
                    c, "DELETE FROM PLAYLIST_LIKE WHERE member_id=? AND playlist_id=?", user, id);
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/dao/SongDao.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.dao;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.Song;

import java.sql.*;
import java.util.*;

public final class SongDao {
    // 검색 조건과 참여자 집계를 분리하여, 아티스트 한 명으로 검색해도 모든 참여자를 표시한다.
    private static final String COLUMNS =
            """
SELECT s.song_id,s.title,
  (SELECT LISTAGG(a.artist_name,', ' ON OVERFLOW TRUNCATE '...' WITHOUT COUNT)
   WITHIN GROUP(ORDER BY a.artist_id) FROM SONG_ARTIST sa JOIN ARTIST a ON a.artist_id=sa.artist_id
   WHERE sa.song_id=s.song_id) artists,s.album_title,s.genre,s.duration_seconds,
""";

    private Song map(ResultSet r) throws SQLException {
        return new Song(
                r.getLong(1),
                r.getString(2),
                r.getString(3),
                r.getString(4),
                r.getString(5),
                r.getInt(6),
                r.getInt(7));
    }

    public List<Song> search(Connection c, String query) throws SQLException {
        if (query.isEmpty())
            return Jdbc.list(c, COLUMNS + " 0 FROM SONG s ORDER BY s.title,s.song_id", this::map);
        return Jdbc.list(
                c,
                COLUMNS
                        + """
                          0 FROM SONG s WHERE INSTR(LOWER(s.title),LOWER(?))>0 OR EXISTS(
                            SELECT 1 FROM SONG_ARTIST sa JOIN ARTIST a ON a.artist_id=sa.artist_id
                            WHERE sa.song_id=s.song_id AND INSTR(LOWER(a.artist_name),LOWER(?))>0)
                          ORDER BY s.title,s.song_id
                          """,
                this::map,
                query,
                query);
    }

    public List<Song> inPlaylist(Connection c, String user, long id) throws SQLException {
        return Jdbc.list(
                c,
                COLUMNS
                        + """
ps.play_order FROM PLAYLIST_SONG ps JOIN SONG s ON s.song_id=ps.song_id
JOIN PLAYLIST p ON p.playlist_id=ps.playlist_id
WHERE ps.playlist_id=? AND (p.member_id=? OR p.is_public='Y') ORDER BY ps.play_order
""",
                this::map,
                id,
                user);
    }

    public void add(Connection c, String user, long id, long song, int order) throws SQLException {
        Jdbc.update(
                c,
                """
                INSERT INTO PLAYLIST_SONG(playlist_id,song_id,play_order)
                SELECT playlist_id,?,? FROM PLAYLIST WHERE playlist_id=? AND member_id=?
                """,
                song,
                order,
                id,
                user);
    }

    public int remove(Connection c, String user, long id, long song) throws SQLException {
        return Jdbc.update(
                c,
                """
DELETE FROM PLAYLIST_SONG ps WHERE playlist_id=? AND song_id=?
AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM PLAYLIST p WHERE p.playlist_id=ps.playlist_id AND p.member_id=?)
""",
                id,
                song,
                user);
    }

    public void order(Connection c, String user, long id, long song, int order)
            throws SQLException {
        int n =
                Jdbc.update(
                        c,
                        """
UPDATE PLAYLIST_SONG ps SET play_order=? WHERE playlist_id=? AND song_id=?
AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM PLAYLIST p WHERE p.playlist_id=ps.playlist_id AND p.member_id=?)
""",
                        order,
                        id,
                        song,
                        user);
        if (n != 1) throw new SQLException("대상 곡이 변경되었습니다. 새로고침 후 다시 시도해 주세요.");
    }
}
```

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model;

public record MediaTrack(

    public MediaTrack(
            Song song, String resource, String performer, String sourceUrl, String license) {
        this(song, resource, performer, sourceUrl, license, 0, null, null);

    public boolean playable() {
        return resource != null && !resource.isBlank();
```

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

public record PassStatus(LocalDateTime expiresAt, LocalDateTime databaseNow) {
    public boolean active() {
        return expiresAt != null && expiresAt.isAfter(databaseNow);
    }

    public int limitSeconds(int duration) {
        return active() ? duration : Math.min(60, duration);
    }

    public static PassStatus free() {
        return new PassStatus(null, LocalDateTime.now());
    }
}
```

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.security;

import java.security.*;
import java.util.*;

import javax.crypto.SecretKeyFactory;
import javax.crypto.spec.PBEKeySpec;

public final class PasswordHasher {
    private static final int ITERATIONS = 600000;

    private PasswordHasher() {}

    public static String hash(char[] password) {
        byte[] salt = new byte[16];
        new SecureRandom().nextBytes(salt);
        return "pbkdf2_sha256$"
                + ITERATIONS
                + "$"
                + Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(salt)
                + "$"
                + Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(derive(password, salt, ITERATIONS));
    }

    public static boolean verify(char[] password, String encoded) {
        if (encoded == null) return false;
        try {
            String[] parts = encoded.split("\\$");
            if (parts.length != 4 || !parts[0].equals("pbkdf2_sha256")) return false;
            int count = Integer.parseInt(parts[1]);
            if (count < 100000 || count > 2000000) return false;
            byte[] salt = Base64.getDecoder().decode(parts[2]),
                    expected = Base64.getDecoder().decode(parts[3]);
            return salt.length == 16
                    && expected.length == 32
                    && MessageDigest.isEqual(expected, derive(password, salt, count));
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    private static byte[] derive(char[] password, byte[] salt, int count) {
        PBEKeySpec spec = new PBEKeySpec(password, salt, count, 256);
        try {
            return SecretKeyFactory.getInstance("PBKDF2WithHmacSHA256")
                    .generateSecret(spec)
                    .getEncoded();
        } catch (GeneralSecurityException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("비밀번호 암호화 기능을 사용할 수 없습니다.", e);
        } finally {
            spec.clearPassword();
        }
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/service/AuthService.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.config.Database;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.dao.MemberDao;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.Member;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.security.PasswordHasher;

import java.sql.*;
import java.util.Arrays;

public final class AuthService {
    private final MemberDao dao = new MemberDao();

    public static String text(String s, String label, int max, boolean required) {
        String v = s == null ? "" : s.strip();
        if (required && v.isEmpty()) throw new AppException(label + "을(를) 입력해 주세요.");
        if (v.codePointCount(0, v.length()) > max)
            throw new AppException(label + "은(는) " + max + "자 이하여야 합니다.");
        return v;
    }

    public boolean available(String id) throws SQLException {
        id = text(id, "아이디", 30, true);
        try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
            return dao.find(c, id).isEmpty();
        }
    }

    public void register(String id, char[] password, char[] confirm, String nickname, String email)
            throws SQLException {
        try {
            id = text(id, "아이디", 30, true);
            nickname = text(nickname, "닉네임", 50, true);
            email = text(email, "이메일", 254, true);
            if (password.length == 0) throw new AppException("비밀번호를 입력해 주세요.");
            if (password.length > 1024) throw new AppException("비밀번호는 1024자 이하여야 합니다.");
            if (!Arrays.equals(password, confirm)) throw new AppException("비밀번호 확인이 일치하지 않습니다.");
            String hash = PasswordHasher.hash(password);
            try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
                if (dao.find(c, id).isPresent()) throw new AppException("이미 사용 중인 아이디입니다.");
                dao.insert(c, new Member(id, nickname, email), hash);
            } catch (SQLException e) {
                if (e.getErrorCode() == 1) throw new AppException("이미 사용 중인 아이디입니다.");
                throw e;
            }
        } finally {
            Arrays.fill(password, '\0');
            Arrays.fill(confirm, '\0');
        }
    }

    public Member login(String id, char[] password) throws SQLException {
        try {
            id = text(id, "아이디", 30, true);
            try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
                var found = dao.find(c, id);
                if (found.isEmpty() || !PasswordHasher.verify(password, found.get().hash()))
                    throw new AppException("아이디 또는 비밀번호가 올바르지 않습니다. 예시 계정 대신 새로 회원가입해 주세요.");
                return found.get().member();
            }
        } finally {
            Arrays.fill(password, '\0');
        }
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/service/LocalAudioService.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.config.Database;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.dao.Jdbc;

import java.nio.file.*;
import java.sql.*;
import java.util.UUID;

import javax.sound.sampled.*;

/** User-owned MP3 files stay outside the public repository. */
public final class LocalAudioService {
    public void attach(long songId, Path source) throws Exception {
        if (!source.getFileName().toString().toLowerCase().endsWith(".mp3")
                || Files.size(source) > 100_000_000)
            throw new AppException("100MB 이하의 MP3 파일을 선택해 주세요.");
        Path folder = Path.of("config/audio");
        Files.createDirectories(folder);
        String name = UUID.randomUUID() + ".mp3";
        Path target = folder.resolve(name);
        Files.copy(source, target);
        boolean saved = false;
        try {
            int seconds = duration(target);
            if (seconds <= 0) throw new AppException("재생 길이를 확인할 수 없는 음원입니다.");
            try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
                c.setAutoCommit(false);
                try {
                    if (Jdbc.list(
                                    c,
                                    "SELECT song_id FROM SONG WHERE song_id=? FOR UPDATE WAIT 5",
                                    r -> r.getLong(1),
                                    songId)
                            .isEmpty()) throw new AppException("등록된 곡을 찾을 수 없습니다.");
                    Jdbc.update(
                            c,
                            "MERGE INTO SONG_MEDIA d USING(SELECT ? id FROM DUAL) x"
                                + " ON(d.song_id=x.id) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET"
                                + " resource_path=?,performer='사용자 연결"
                                + " 음원',source_url='local:private',license_name='사용자 보유 음원' WHEN"
                                + " NOT MATCHED THEN"
                                + " INSERT(song_id,resource_path,performer,source_url,license_name)"
                                + " VALUES(x.id,?,'사용자 연결 음원','local:private','사용자 보유 음원')",
                            songId,
                            "local-audio/" + name,
                            "local-audio/" + name);
                    Jdbc.update(
                            c,
                            "UPDATE SONG SET duration_seconds=? WHERE song_id=?",
                            seconds,
                            songId);
                    c.commit();
                    saved = true;
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    c.rollback();
                    throw e;
                }
            }
        } finally {
            if (!saved) Files.deleteIfExists(target);
        }
    }

    private int duration(Path path) throws Exception {
        try (AudioInputStream input = AudioSystem.getAudioInputStream(path.toFile())) {
            AudioFormat f = input.getFormat();
            AudioFormat pcm =
                    new AudioFormat(
                            AudioFormat.Encoding.PCM_SIGNED,
                            f.getSampleRate(),
                            16,
                            f.getChannels(),
                            f.getChannels() * 2,
                            f.getSampleRate(),
                            false);
            try (AudioInputStream decoded = AudioSystem.getAudioInputStream(pcm, input)) {
                byte[] buffer = new byte[32768];
                long bytes = 0;
                int n;
                while ((n = decoded.read(buffer)) >= 0) bytes += n;
                return (int) Math.ceil(bytes / (pcm.getFrameSize() * pcm.getFrameRate()));
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/service/LocalProfileService.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.Member;

import java.io.*;
import java.nio.channels.*;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.*;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.*;

/** 로그인 UI 없이 이 PC 전용 계정을 만들되, 정상 PBKDF2 인증을 거쳐 소유권을 유지한다. */
public final class LocalProfileService {
    public Member resolve() throws IOException, SQLException {
        return resolve(
                Path.of(System.getProperty("playlist.profile", "config/local-profile.properties")));
    }

    public Member resolve(Path file) throws IOException, SQLException {
        file = file.toAbsolutePath();
        Files.createDirectories(file.getParent());
        try (FileChannel channel =
                        FileChannel.open(
                                file.resolveSibling("local-profile.lock"),
                                StandardOpenOption.CREATE,
                                StandardOpenOption.WRITE);
                FileLock lock = channel.lock()) {
            Properties p = new Properties();
            if (Files.exists(file))
                try (Reader in = Files.newBufferedReader(file, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
                    p.load(in);
                }
            else {
                p.setProperty(
                        "member.id",
                        "local_" + UUID.randomUUID().toString().replace("-", "").substring(0, 20));
                p.setProperty("member.secret", UUID.randomUUID().toString());
                try (Writer out =
                        Files.newBufferedWriter(
                                file, StandardCharsets.UTF_8, StandardOpenOption.CREATE_NEW)) {
                    p.store(out, "Private local profile. Do not upload.");
                }
            }
            String id = p.getProperty("member.id", ""), secret = p.getProperty("member.secret", "");
            if (!id.matches("local_[a-f0-9]{20}") || secret.length() < 32)
                throw new AppException(
                        "개인 보관함 설정이 손상되었습니다. 기존 local-profile.properties 파일을 확인해 주세요.");
            AuthService auth = new AuthService();
            if (auth.available(id))
                auth.register(
                        id,
                        secret.toCharArray(),
                        secret.toCharArray(),
                        "나의 보관함",
                        id + "@local.invalid");
            return auth.login(id, secret.toCharArray());
        }
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/service/MelonChartService.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.config.Database;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.dao.Jdbc;

import org.jsoup.Jsoup;
import org.jsoup.nodes.*;

import java.nio.file.*;
import java.sql.*;
import java.time.*;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.regex.*;

/** Public chart metadata only. No streaming URLs or audio are collected. */
public final class MelonChartService {
    public static final String SOURCE = "https://www.melon.com/chart/index.htm";

    public record Artist(long id, String name) {}

    public record Entry(
            int rank,
            long melonId,
            String title,
            String album,
            String cover,
            List<Artist> artists) {
        public long songId() {
            return 1_000_000_000L + melonId;
        }
    }

    public record Snapshot(LocalDateTime chartAt, List<Entry> entries) {}

    public Snapshot fetch() throws Exception {
        return parse(
                Jsoup.connect(SOURCE)
                        .userAgent("Mozilla/5.0")
                        .referrer("https://www.melon.com/")
                        .timeout(30000)
                        .get());
    }

    public Snapshot read(Path path) throws Exception {
        return parse(Jsoup.parse(path.toFile(), "UTF-8", SOURCE));
    }

    public Snapshot parse(Document document) {
        try {
            LocalDateTime at =
                    LocalDateTime.parse(
                            document.selectFirst(".year").text()
                                    + " "
                                    + document.selectFirst(".hour").text(),
                            DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy.MM.dd HH:mm"));
            List<Entry> entries = new ArrayList<>();
            Set<Long> songs = new HashSet<>();
            for (Element row : document.select("tr.lst50[data-song-no], tr.lst100[data-song-no]")) {
                int rank = Integer.parseInt(row.selectFirst(".rank").text());
                long id = Long.parseLong(row.attr("data-song-no"));
                String title = row.selectFirst(".rank01 a").text();
                String album = row.selectFirst(".rank03 a").text();
                Map<Long, Artist> artists = new LinkedHashMap<>();
                for (Element a : row.select(".rank02 a[href*=artistId]")) {
                    Matcher matcher =
                            Pattern.compile("artistId[=\\s]*([0-9]+)").matcher(a.attr("href"));
                    if (matcher.find()) {
                        long artistId = Long.parseLong(matcher.group(1));
                        artists.putIfAbsent(
                                artistId, new Artist(2_000_000_000L + artistId, a.text()));
                    }
                }
                if (rank != entries.size() + 1
                        || !songs.add(id)
                        || title.isBlank()
                        || album.isBlank()
                        || artists.isEmpty())
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException("차트 순위·중복·곡 정보 검증 실패");
                entries.add(
                        new Entry(
                                rank,
                                id,
                                title,
                                album,
                                row.selectFirst("img").absUrl("src"),
                                List.copyOf(artists.values())));
            }
            if (entries.size() != 100) throw new IllegalArgumentException("TOP100 전체 100곡이 필요합니다.");
            return new Snapshot(at, List.copyOf(entries));
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            throw new AppException("멜론 차트 형식이 바뀌었거나 100곡을 확인할 수 없습니다. 기존 DB 목록은 유지됩니다.");
        }
    }

    public void save(Snapshot snapshot) throws SQLException {
        if (snapshot.entries().size() != 100) throw new AppException("100곡을 확인한 차트만 저장할 수 있습니다.");
        try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
            c.setAutoCommit(false);
            try {
                Jdbc.update(c, "LOCK TABLE MELON_CHART IN EXCLUSIVE MODE WAIT 5");
                Timestamp at = Timestamp.valueOf(snapshot.chartAt());
                for (Entry e : snapshot.entries()) {
                    for (Artist a : e.artists()) {
                        Jdbc.update(
                                c,
                                "MERGE INTO ARTIST d USING(SELECT ? id FROM DUAL) x"
                                    + " ON(d.artist_id=x.id) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET"
                                    + " artist_name=? WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN"
                                    + " INSERT(artist_id,artist_name,country) VALUES(x.id,?,'미확인')",
                                a.id(),
                                a.name(),
                                a.name());
                    }
                    Jdbc.update(
                            c,
                            "MERGE INTO SONG d USING(SELECT ? id FROM DUAL) x ON(d.song_id=x.id)"
                                + " WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET title=?,album_title=? WHEN NOT"
                                + " MATCHED THEN INSERT(song_id,title,album_title)"
                                + " VALUES(x.id,?,?)",
                            e.songId(),
                            e.title(),
                            e.album(),
                            e.title(),
                            e.album());
                    for (Artist a : e.artists())
                        Jdbc.update(
                                c,
                                "MERGE INTO SONG_ARTIST d USING(SELECT ? s,? a FROM DUAL) x"
                                    + " ON(d.song_id=x.s AND d.artist_id=x.a) WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN"
                                    + " INSERT(song_id,artist_id) VALUES(x.s,x.a)",
                                e.songId(),
                                a.id());
                }
                Jdbc.update(
                        c,
                        "MERGE INTO MELON_CHART d USING(SELECT ? at FROM DUAL) x"
                            + " ON(d.chart_at=x.at) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET"
                            + " fetched_at=SYSTIMESTAMP WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN"
                            + " INSERT(chart_at,source_url,row_count) VALUES(x.at,?,100)",
                        at,
                        SOURCE);
                for (Entry e : snapshot.entries())
                    Jdbc.update(
                            c,
                            "MERGE INTO MELON_CHART_ENTRY d USING(SELECT ? at,? rank FROM DUAL) x"
                                + " ON(d.chart_at=x.at AND d.chart_rank=x.rank) WHEN MATCHED THEN"
                                + " UPDATE SET song_id=?,melon_song_id=?,cover_url=? WHEN NOT"
                                + " MATCHED THEN"
                                + " INSERT(chart_at,chart_rank,song_id,melon_song_id,cover_url)"
                                + " VALUES(x.at,x.rank,?,?,?)",
                            at,
                            e.rank(),
                            e.songId(),
                            e.melonId(),
                            e.cover(),
                            e.songId(),
                            e.melonId(),
                            e.cover());
                c.commit();
            } catch (Exception e) {
                c.rollback();
                throw e;
            }
        }
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
        MelonChartService service = new MelonChartService();
        Snapshot snapshot = args.length == 0 ? service.fetch() : service.read(Path.of(args[0]));
        service.save(snapshot);
        System.out.println(
                "Oracle Melon TOP100: "
                        + snapshot.chartAt()
                        + " / "
                        + snapshot.entries().size()
                        + " songs");
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/service/MembershipService.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.config.Database;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.dao.Jdbc;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.*;

import java.sql.*;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.*;

/** 실청구가 없는 과제용 결제. 상태의 원본은 Oracle이며 파일에 유료 상태를 저장하지 않는다. */
public final class MembershipService {
    public static final int PRICE_WON = 4900;
    private final String member;

    public MembershipService(Member member) {
        this.member = member.id();
    }

    public PassStatus status() throws SQLException {
        try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
            return status(c);
        }
    }

    private PassStatus status(Connection c) throws SQLException {
        return Jdbc.list(
                        c,
                        """
SELECT (SELECT MAX(valid_until) FROM MEMBERSHIP_ORDER WHERE member_id=? AND status='PAID'),
CAST(SYSTIMESTAMP AS TIMESTAMP) FROM DUAL
""",
                        r ->
                                new PassStatus(
                                        r.getTimestamp(1) == null
                                                ? null
                                                : r.getTimestamp(1).toLocalDateTime(),
                                        r.getTimestamp(2).toLocalDateTime()),
                        member)
                .get(0);
    }

    public List<PaymentOrder> history() throws SQLException {
        try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
            return Jdbc.list(
                    c,
                    "SELECT order_id,amount_won,paid_at,valid_until,status FROM MEMBERSHIP_ORDER"
                            + " WHERE member_id=? ORDER BY paid_at DESC,order_id",
                    r ->
                            new PaymentOrder(
                                    r.getString(1),
                                    r.getInt(2),
                                    r.getTimestamp(3).toLocalDateTime(),
                                    r.getTimestamp(4).toLocalDateTime(),
                                    r.getString(5)),
                    member);
        }
    }

    public void purchase(String requestId) throws SQLException {
        UUID.fromString(requestId);
        try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
            c.setAutoCommit(false);
            try {
                lockMember(c);
                var existing =
                        Jdbc.list(
                                c,
                                "SELECT member_id FROM MEMBERSHIP_ORDER WHERE order_id=?",
                                r -> r.getString(1),
                                requestId);
                if (!existing.isEmpty()) {
                    if (!member.equals(existing.get(0)))
                        throw new AppException("다른 보관함의 결제 요청입니다.");
                    c.rollback();
                    return;
                }
                PassStatus current = status(c);
                LocalDateTime start =
                        current.active() ? current.expiresAt() : current.databaseNow();
                Jdbc.update(
                        c,
                        "INSERT INTO"
                            + " MEMBERSHIP_ORDER(order_id,member_id,amount_won,valid_until,status,payment_method)"
                            + " VALUES(?,?,?,?,'PAID','DEMO')",
                        requestId,
                        member,
                        PRICE_WON,
                        Timestamp.valueOf(start.plusDays(30)));
                c.commit();
            } catch (SQLException | RuntimeException e) {
                c.rollback();
                throw e;
            }
        }
    }

    public void cancelLatest() throws SQLException {
        try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
            c.setAutoCommit(false);
            try {
                lockMember(c);
                var ids =
                        Jdbc.list(
                                c,
                                "SELECT order_id FROM MEMBERSHIP_ORDER WHERE member_id=? AND"
                                        + " status='PAID' ORDER BY valid_until DESC,paid_at DESC",
                                r -> r.getString(1),
                                member);
                if (ids.isEmpty()) throw new AppException("취소할 이용권이 없습니다.");
                Jdbc.update(
                        c,
                        "UPDATE MEMBERSHIP_ORDER SET status='CANCELLED' WHERE order_id=? AND"
                                + " member_id=? AND status='PAID'",
                        ids.get(0),
                        member);
                c.commit();
            } catch (SQLException | RuntimeException e) {
                c.rollback();
                throw e;
            }
        }
    }

    private void lockMember(Connection c) throws SQLException {
        if (Jdbc.list(
                        c,
                        "SELECT member_id FROM MEMBER WHERE member_id=? FOR UPDATE WAIT 5",
                        r -> r.getString(1),
                        member)
                .isEmpty()) throw new AppException("개인 보관함을 찾을 수 없습니다.");
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/service/MusicLibraryService.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.config.Database;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.dao.MediaDao;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.*;

import java.sql.*;
import java.util.*;

public final class MusicLibraryService {
    private final MediaDao media = new MediaDao();
    private final MembershipService membership;

    public MusicLibraryService(Member member) {
        membership = new MembershipService(member);
    }

    public List<MediaTrack> catalog() throws SQLException {
        try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
            return media.catalog(c);
        }
    }

    public record Playback(MediaTrack track, PassStatus pass) {}

    public Playback authorize(long songId) throws SQLException {
        MediaTrack track;
        try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
            track = media.find(c, songId);
        }
        if (track == null || !track.playable())
            throw new AppException("곡 정보만 등록되어 있습니다. 재생하려면 사용 권한이 있는 MP3 음원을 연결해 주세요.");
        if (!track.resource().matches("audio/[a-z0-9-]+[.]mp3|local-audio/[a-f0-9-]+[.]mp3"))
            throw new AppException("음원 경로가 올바르지 않습니다.");
        return new Playback(track, membership.status());
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/service/PlaylistService.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.config.Database;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.dao.*;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.*;

import java.sql.*;
import java.util.*;

/** 로그인 회원마다 생성한다. 모든 쓰기 작업은 부모 PLAYLIST 행 잠금과 같은 연결을 사용한다. */
public final class PlaylistService {
    private final Member member;
    private final PlaylistDao playlists = new PlaylistDao();
    private final SongDao songs = new SongDao();

    public PlaylistService(Member member) {
        this.member = Objects.requireNonNull(member);
    }

    public Member member() {
        return member;
    }

    @FunctionalInterface
    private interface Work<T> {
        T run(Connection c) throws SQLException;
    }

    private <T> T transaction(Work<T> action) throws SQLException {
        try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
            c.setAutoCommit(false);
            try {
                T value = action.run(c);
                c.commit();
                return value;
            } catch (SQLException | RuntimeException e) {
                try {
                    c.rollback();
                } catch (SQLException rollback) {
                    e.addSuppressed(rollback);
                }
                throw e;
            }
        }
    }

    private void owner(Connection c, long id) throws SQLException {
        if (!playlists.lockOwner(c, member.id(), id))
            throw new AppException("본인이 만든 플레이리스트만 변경할 수 있습니다.");
    }

    public List<Playlist> mine() throws SQLException {
        try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
            return playlists.mine(c, member.id());
        }
    }

    public List<Playlist> discover(String query) throws SQLException {
        String q = AuthService.text(query, "검색어", 150, false);
        try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
            return q.isEmpty()
                    ? playlists.allPublic(c, member.id())
                    : playlists.publicLists(c, member.id(), q);
        }
    }

    public List<Song> search(String query) throws SQLException {
        String q = AuthService.text(query, "검색어", 200, false);
        try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
            return songs.search(c, q);
        }
    }

    public List<Song> searchPlayable(String query) throws SQLException {
        String q = AuthService.text(query, "검색어", 200, false).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
            return new MediaDao()
                    .catalog(c).stream()
                            .map(MediaTrack::song)
                            .filter(
                                    song ->
                                            (song.title() + " " + song.artists())
                                                    .toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)
                                                    .contains(q))
                            .toList();
        }
    }

    public PlaylistDetail detail(long id) throws SQLException {
        try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
            // 두 SELECT가 동일 시점의 공개 여부와 수록곡을 보도록 읽기 전용 트랜잭션 사용.
            c.setAutoCommit(false);
            try {
                Jdbc.update(c, "SET TRANSACTION READ ONLY");
                Playlist p =
                        playlists
                                .visible(c, member.id(), id)
                                .orElseThrow(() -> new AppException("조회할 수 없는 플레이리스트입니다."));
                var list = songs.inPlaylist(c, member.id(), id);
                c.commit();
                return new PlaylistDetail(p, List.copyOf(list));
            } catch (SQLException | RuntimeException e) {
                c.rollback();
                throw e;
            }
        }
    }

    public long create(String title, String description, boolean pub) throws SQLException {
        String t = AuthService.text(title, "제목", 150, true),
                d = AuthService.text(description, "설명", 1000, false);
        return transaction(c -> playlists.create(c, member.id(), t, d, pub));
    }

    public void edit(long id, String title, String description, boolean pub) throws SQLException {
        String t = AuthService.text(title, "제목", 150, true),
                d = AuthService.text(description, "설명", 1000, false);
        transaction(
                c -> {
                    owner(c, id);
                    playlists.edit(c, member.id(), id, t, d, pub);
                    return null;
                });
    }

    public void delete(long id) throws SQLException {
        transaction(
                c -> {
                    owner(c, id);
                    playlists.delete(c, member.id(), id);
                    return null;
                });
    }

    public void add(long id, long song) throws SQLException {
        transaction(
                c -> {
                    owner(c, id);
                    var list = songs.inPlaylist(c, member.id(), id);
                    if (list.stream().anyMatch(s -> s.id() == song))
                        throw new AppException("이미 추가한 곡입니다.");
                    int next = list.stream().mapToInt(Song::order).max().orElse(0) + 1;
                    if (next > 999999) throw new AppException("목록에 더 이상 곡을 추가할 수 없습니다.");
                    songs.add(c, member.id(), id, song, next);
                    return null;
                });
    }

    public void remove(long id, long song) throws SQLException {
        transaction(
                c -> {
                    owner(c, id);
                    if (songs.remove(c, member.id(), id, song) != 1)
                        throw new AppException("이미 제거된 곡입니다.");
                    var remaining = songs.inPlaylist(c, member.id(), id);
                    // 오름차순으로 빈 번호에 이동하므로 UNIQUE 위반 없이 1부터 정리된다.
                    for (int i = 0; i < remaining.size(); i++)
                        if (remaining.get(i).order() != i + 1)
                            songs.order(c, member.id(), id, remaining.get(i).id(), i + 1);
                    return null;
                });
    }

    public void move(long id, long song, int direction) throws SQLException {
        if (direction != 1 && direction != -1) throw new AppException("이동 방향이 올바르지 않습니다.");
        transaction(
                c -> {
                    owner(c, id);
                    var list = songs.inPlaylist(c, member.id(), id);
                    int index = -1;
                    for (int i = 0; i < list.size(); i++) if (list.get(i).id() == song) index = i;
                    if (index < 0) throw new AppException("목록에 없는 곡입니다.");
                    int other = index + direction;
                    if (other < 0 || other >= list.size()) return null;
                    Set<Integer> occupied = new HashSet<>();
                    for (Song s : list) occupied.add(s.order());
                    int temp = 1;
                    while (occupied.contains(temp) && temp <= 999999) temp++;
                    if (temp > 999999)
                        throw new AppException("순서를 바꿀 빈 번호가 없습니다. 곡 하나를 제거한 후 다시 시도해 주세요.");
                    Song a = list.get(index), b = list.get(other);
                    songs.order(c, member.id(), id, a.id(), temp);
                    songs.order(c, member.id(), id, b.id(), a.order());
                    songs.order(c, member.id(), id, a.id(), b.order());
                    return null;
                });
    }

    public void like(long id, boolean value) throws SQLException {
        transaction(
                c -> {
                    if (value && !playlists.lockPublic(c, id))
                        throw new AppException("공개 플레이리스트에만 좋아요를 누를 수 있습니다.");
                    playlists.like(c, member.id(), id, value);
                    return null;
                });
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/ui/AlbumArt.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.ui;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.MediaTrack;

import java.awt.*;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.net.*;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.concurrent.*;

import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import javax.swing.*;

/** Album thumbnails load off the EDT; missing images use the app's own artwork. */
final class AlbumArt implements Icon {
    private static final Map<String, BufferedImage> CACHE = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private static final Set<String> PENDING = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
    private static final ExecutorService FETCH =
            Executors.newFixedThreadPool(
                    3,
                    r -> {
                        Thread t = new Thread(r, "album-art");
                        t.setDaemon(true);
                        return t;
                    });
    private final MediaTrack track;
    private final int size;

    AlbumArt(MediaTrack track, int size) {
        this.track = track;
        this.size = size;
    }

    public int getIconWidth() {
        return size;
    }

    public int getIconHeight() {
        return size;
    }

    public void paintIcon(Component c, Graphics g, int x, int y) {
        String url = track.coverUrl();
        BufferedImage image = url == null ? null : CACHE.get(url);
        if (image != null) g.drawImage(image, x, y, size, size, null);
        else {
            new MusicArt(track.song().id(), size).paintIcon(c, g, x, y);
            if (url != null && PENDING.add(url))
                FETCH.submit(
                        () -> {
                            try {
                                URI uri = URI.create(url);
                                if (!"https".equals(uri.getScheme())
                                        || !"cdnimg.melon.co.kr".equals(uri.getHost())) return;
                                URLConnection connection = uri.toURL().openConnection();
                                connection.setConnectTimeout(6000);
                                connection.setReadTimeout(6000);
                                try (var input = connection.getInputStream()) {
                                    BufferedImage loaded = ImageIO.read(input);
                                    if (loaded != null) CACHE.put(url, loaded);
                                }
                            } catch (Exception ignored) {
                            } finally {
                                SwingUtilities.invokeLater(c::repaint);
                            }
                        });
        }
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/ui/ConnectionDialog.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.ui;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.config.Database;

import java.awt.*;

import javax.swing.*;

public final class ConnectionDialog {
    private ConnectionDialog() {}

    public static void open(Window owner, Runnable connected) {
        JDialog dialog = new JDialog(owner, "음악 보관함 연결", Dialog.ModalityType.APPLICATION_MODAL);
        JPanel p = Ui.padded(28);
        JPanel form = Ui.column();
        form.add(Ui.text("Oracle 보관함 연결", 23, true));
        form.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(8));
        form.add(Ui.muted("한 번 연결하면 다음부터 바로 음악 보관함이 열립니다."));
        form.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(22));
        var properties = Database.settings();
        JTextField
                url =
                        new JTextField(
                                properties.getProperty(
                                        "db.url", "jdbc:oracle:thin:@localhost:1521:xe"),
                                35),
                user = new JTextField(properties.getProperty("db.username", "c##dbsoft"), 35);
        JPasswordField password = new JPasswordField(properties.getProperty("db.password", ""), 35);
        for (var entry :
                new Object[][] {
                    {"JDBC URL", url}, {"Oracle 사용자 이름", user}, {"Oracle 비밀번호", password}
                }) {
            form.add(Ui.muted((String) entry[0]));
            form.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(6));
            form.add((Component) entry[1]);
            form.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(15));
        }
        form.add(Ui.muted("연결 전 sql/02와 sql/06 파일을 SQL Developer에서 실행해 주세요."));
        p.add(form, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        p.add(
                Ui.row(
                        Ui.primary(
                                "연결 확인하고 저장",
                                () -> {
                                    String u = url.getText(), name = user.getText();
                                    char[] secret = password.getPassword();
                                    Ui.work(
                                            dialog,
                                            () -> {
                                                Database.testAndSave(u, name, secret);
                                                return true;
                                            },
                                            ok -> {
                                                dialog.dispose();
                                                connected.run();
                                            });
                                }),
                        Ui.quiet("닫기", dialog::dispose)),
                BorderLayout.SOUTH);
        dialog.setContentPane(p);
        dialog.pack();
        dialog.setMinimumSize(dialog.getSize());
        dialog.setLocationRelativeTo(owner);
        dialog.setVisible(true);
    }
}
```
