## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/ui/DetailDialog.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.ui;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.*;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service.*;

import java.awt.*;
import java.util.List;

import javax.swing.*;
import javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel;

public final class DetailDialog extends JDialog {
    private final PlaylistService service;
    private final java.util.function.Consumer<Song> listen;
    private final long id;
    private final Runnable changed;
    private PlaylistDetail detail;
    private List<Song> searchResults = List.of();
    private final JLabel title = Ui.heading("플레이리스트 불러오는 중"), meta = new JLabel(" ");
    private final JTextArea description = new JTextArea(2, 30);
    private final DefaultTableModel model = Ui.model("순서", "곡 제목", "아티스트", "앨범", "장르", "재생시간");
    private final DefaultTableModel searchModel = Ui.model("곡 제목", "아티스트", "앨범", "장르", "재생시간");
    private final JTable table = Ui.table(model), searchTable = Ui.table(searchModel);
    private final JPanel manage = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(0, 8));
    private final JButton like = Ui.button("좋아요", this::like);
    private final JTextField query = new JTextField(25);

    public DetailDialog(Window owner, PlaylistService service, long id, Runnable changed) {
        this(owner, service, id, changed, song -> {});
    }

    public DetailDialog(
            Window owner,
            PlaylistService service,
            long id,
            Runnable changed,
            java.util.function.Consumer<Song> listen) {
        super(owner, "플레이리스트 상세", ModalityType.APPLICATION_MODAL);
        this.listen = listen;
        this.service = service;
        this.id = id;
        this.changed = changed;
        setDefaultCloseOperation(DISPOSE_ON_CLOSE);
        setSize(1100, 790);
        setMinimumSize(new Dimension(850, 620));
        JPanel root = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(0, 14));
        root.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(22, 26, 22, 26));
        JPanel heading = Ui.column();
        heading.add(title);
        heading.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(10));
        heading.add(meta);
        heading.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(10));
        description.setEditable(false);
        description.setFocusable(false);
        description.setLineWrap(true);
        description.setWrapStyleWord(true);
        heading.add(new JScrollPane(description));
        JPanel header = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(22, 0));
        header.add(new MusicArt(id, 115), BorderLayout.WEST);
        header.add(heading, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        root.add(header, BorderLayout.NORTH);
        JPanel contents = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(0, 8));
        contents.add(new JScrollPane(table), BorderLayout.CENTER);
        JPanel ownButtons =
                Ui.row(
                        Ui.button("선택 곡 제거", () -> modify(0)),
                        Ui.button("위로 이동", () -> modify(-1)),
                        Ui.button("아래로 이동", () -> modify(1)));
        manage.add(ownButtons, BorderLayout.NORTH);
        JPanel search = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(0, 8));
        search.add(
                Ui.row(
                        new JLabel("곡 제목 / 아티스트"),
                        query,
                        Ui.button("곡 검색", this::search),
                        Ui.button("선택 곡 추가", this::add)),
                BorderLayout.NORTH);
        JScrollPane results = new JScrollPane(searchTable);
        results.setPreferredSize(new Dimension(700, 175));
        search.add(results, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        manage.add(search, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        contents.add(manage, BorderLayout.SOUTH);
        root.add(contents, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        root.add(
                Ui.row(
                        Ui.primary(
                                "선택 곡 듣기",
                                () -> {
                                    int row = table.getSelectedRow();
                                    if (row >= 0 && detail != null)
                                        listen.accept(detail.songs().get(row));
                                }),
                        like,
                        Ui.button("새로고침", this::reload),
                        Ui.button("닫기", this::dispose)),
                BorderLayout.SOUTH);
        manage.setVisible(false);
        like.setEnabled(false);
        setContentPane(root);
        setLocationRelativeTo(owner);
        query.addActionListener(e -> search());
        SwingUtilities.invokeLater(this::reload);
    }

    public void reload() {
        Ui.work(
                this,
                () -> service.detail(id),
                data -> {
                    detail = data;
                    Playlist p = data.playlist();
                    title.setText(p.title());
                    meta.setText(
                            "작성자: "
                                    + p.nickname()
                                    + "    |    "
                                    + (p.isPublic() ? "공개" : "비공개")
                                    + "    |    좋아요 "
                                    + p.likes()
                                    + "개");
                    description.setText(Ui.safe(p.description()));
                    model.setRowCount(0);
                    for (Song s : data.songs())
                        model.addRow(
                                new Object[] {
                                    s.order(),
                                    s.title(),
                                    s.artists(),
                                    s.album(),
                                    s.genre(),
                                    s.duration()
                                });
                    boolean own = p.owner().equals(service.member().id());
                    manage.setVisible(own);
                    like.setEnabled(p.isPublic() || p.liked());
                    like.setText(p.liked() ? "좋아요 취소" : "좋아요");
                    revalidate();
                    if (own && searchResults.isEmpty()) search();
                });
    }

    private void search() {
        String q = query.getText();
        Ui.work(
                this,
                () -> service.searchPlayable(q),
                data -> {
                    searchResults = data;
                    searchModel.setRowCount(0);
                    for (Song s : data)
                        searchModel.addRow(
                                new Object[] {
                                    s.title(), s.artists(), s.album(), s.genre(), s.duration()
                                });
                });
    }

    private void add() {
        int row = searchTable.getSelectedRow();
        if (row < 0) {
            Ui.info(this, "추가할 검색 결과 곡을 선택해 주세요.");
            return;
        }
        long song = searchResults.get(searchTable.convertRowIndexToModel(row)).id();
        Ui.work(
                this,
                () -> {
                    service.add(id, song);
                    return true;
                },
                ok -> {
                    reload();
                    changed.run();
                });
    }

    private void modify(int direction) {
        int row = table.getSelectedRow();
        if (row < 0 || detail == null) {
            Ui.info(this, "수록 곡을 선택해 주세요.");
            return;
        }
        long song = detail.songs().get(table.convertRowIndexToModel(row)).id();
        Ui.work(
                this,
                () -> {
                    if (direction == 0) service.remove(id, song);
                    else service.move(id, song, direction);
                    return true;
                },
                ok -> {
                    reload();
                    changed.run();
                });
    }

    private void like() {
        if (detail == null) return;
        boolean value = !detail.playlist().liked();
        Ui.work(
                this,
                () -> {
                    service.like(id, value);
                    return true;
                },
                ok -> {
                    reload();
                    changed.run();
                });
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/ui/MainFrame.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.ui;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.audio.AudioPlayer;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.*;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service.*;

import java.awt.*;
import java.awt.event.*;
import java.awt.geom.*;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.List;

import javax.swing.*;
import javax.swing.table.*;

/** 로그인 화면 없이 열리는 음악 앱. 목록·이용권·저장은 모두 Oracle 서비스에서 읽는다. */
public final class MainFrame extends JFrame {
    private Member member;
    private PlaylistService playlists;
    private MusicLibraryService library;
    private MembershipService membership;
    private List<MediaTrack> tracks = List.of(), shownTracks = List.of();
    private List<Playlist> mine = List.of(), shared = List.of();
    private PassStatus pass = PassStatus.free();
    private final AudioPlayer player = new AudioPlayer();
    private final JPanel content = new JPanel(new BorderLayout()), sidebar = Ui.column();
    private final JLabel connection = Ui.muted("보관함을 연결하는 중입니다."),
            sectionTitle = Ui.text("둘러보기", 15, true);
    private final JTextField search = new JTextField(23);
    private final Map<String, JButton> navigation = new LinkedHashMap<>();
    private final JLabel nowTitle = Ui.text("음악을 선택해 주세요", 13, true),
            nowArtist = Ui.muted("좋아하는 곡을 찾아 들어보세요"),
            time = Ui.muted("0:00 / 0:00"),
            playMode = Ui.muted("무료 · 1분 미리듣기");
    private final JLabel nowArt = new JLabel(new MusicArt(0, 48));
    private final JButton playButton, planButton;
    private final JSlider progress = new JSlider(0, 60, 0);
    private final javax.swing.Timer clock;
    private String page = "홈", filter = "전체";
    private boolean loading, updatingSlider, limitAnnounced;
    private MediaTrack selected;

    public MainFrame() {
        this(null);
    }

    public MainFrame(Member initialMember) {
        super("muse · 음악 플레이리스트");
        member = initialMember;
        setDefaultCloseOperation(DISPOSE_ON_CLOSE);
        setSize(1280, 900);
        setMinimumSize(new Dimension(1060, 740));
        JPanel root = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
        root.add(buildSidebar(), BorderLayout.WEST);
        JPanel main = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
        main.add(buildHeader(), BorderLayout.NORTH);
        main.add(content, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        root.add(main, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        playButton =
                iconButton(
                        "play",
                        24,
                        Ui.ACCENT,
                        () -> {
                            if (player.playing()) {
                                player.togglePause();
                            } else if (selected != null) play(selected);
                        });
        planButton = Ui.primary("Plus 이용권", this::openMembership);
        root.add(buildPlayer(), BorderLayout.SOUTH);
        setContentPane(root);
        setLocationRelativeTo(null);
        search.putClientProperty("JTextField.placeholderText", "곡, 가수, 앨범 검색");
        search.putClientProperty("JTextField.leadingIcon", new MuseIcon("search", 16, Ui.MUTED));
        search.addActionListener(
                e -> {
                    page = "전체 곡";
                    render();
                });
        clock = new javax.swing.Timer(200, e -> tick());
        clock.start();
        addWindowListener(
                new WindowAdapter() {
                    @Override
                    public void windowClosed(WindowEvent e) {
                        clock.stop();
                        player.close();
                    }
                });
        render();
        SwingUtilities.invokeLater(this::refresh);
    }

    private JPanel buildSidebar() {
        sidebar.setBackground(new Color(247, 247, 249));
        sidebar.setPreferredSize(new Dimension(226, 650));
        sidebar.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(30, 18, 22, 18));
        JLabel logo = Ui.text("muse.", 36, true);
        logo.setForeground(Ui.ACCENT);
        logo.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(0, 12, 28, 0));
        logo.setAlignmentX(Component.LEFT_ALIGNMENT);
        sidebar.add(logo);
        JLabel tag = Ui.muted("나의 일상에, 음악");
        tag.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(0, 13, 20, 0));
        sidebar.add(tag);
        String[][] items = {
            {"홈", "home"},
            {"전체 곡", "music"},
            {"내 플레이리스트", "library"},
            {"공개 플레이리스트", "search"},
            {"좋아요한 목록", "heart"}
        };
        for (String[] item : items) {
            JButton b = Ui.button(item[0], () -> navigate(item[0]));
            b.setIcon(new MuseIcon(item[1], 19, Ui.MUTED));
            b.setHorizontalAlignment(SwingConstants.LEFT);
            b.setIconTextGap(12);
            b.setMaximumSize(new Dimension(Integer.MAX_VALUE, 46));
            b.setAlignmentX(Component.LEFT_ALIGNMENT);
            navigation.put(item[0], b);
            sidebar.add(b);
            sidebar.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(5));
        }
        sidebar.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(24));
        JButton create = Ui.quiet("새 플레이리스트", this::create);
        create.setIcon(new MuseIcon("plus", 17, Ui.ACCENT));
        create.setBackground(sidebar.getBackground());
        create.setMaximumSize(new Dimension(Integer.MAX_VALUE, 44));
        sidebar.add(create);
        sidebar.add(Box.createVerticalGlue());
        JPanel plus = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(0, 8));
        plus.setBackground(new Color(237, 235, 242));
        plus.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(16, 14, 14, 14));
        plus.setMaximumSize(new Dimension(Integer.MAX_VALUE, 130));
        JPanel words = Ui.column();
        words.setOpaque(false);
        words.add(Ui.text("muse plus", 18, true));
        words.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(8));
        words.add(Ui.muted("1분을 넘어, 음악 끝까지."));
        plus.add(words, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        JButton more = Ui.button("이용권 알아보기", this::openMembership);
        more.setFont(more.getFont().deriveFont(11f));
        plus.add(more, BorderLayout.SOUTH);
        sidebar.add(plus);
        sidebar.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(24));
        sidebar.add(Ui.text("나의 보관함", 13, true));
        sidebar.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(5));
        sidebar.add(Ui.muted("이 PC의 개인 음악 공간"));
        for (Component child : sidebar.getComponents())
            if (child instanceof JComponent component)
                component.setAlignmentX(Component.LEFT_ALIGNMENT);
        return sidebar;
    }

    private JPanel buildHeader() {
        JPanel header = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(15, 0));
        header.setBorder(
                BorderFactory.createCompoundBorder(
                        BorderFactory.createMatteBorder(0, 0, 1, 0, new Color(239, 239, 243)),
                        BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(17, 30, 16, 30)));
        header.add(sectionTitle, BorderLayout.WEST);
        JPanel tools =
                Ui.row(
                        search,
                        Ui.quiet("차트 갱신", this::updateChart),
                        Ui.quiet("연결 설정", () -> ConnectionDialog.open(this, this::refresh)));
        tools.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder());
        header.add(tools, BorderLayout.EAST);
        return header;
    }

    private JPanel buildPlayer() {
        JPanel footer = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(24, 0));
        footer.setPreferredSize(new Dimension(1100, 98));
        footer.setBorder(
                BorderFactory.createCompoundBorder(
                        BorderFactory.createMatteBorder(1, 0, 0, 0, new Color(234, 234, 240)),
                        BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(15, 24, 14, 24)));
        JPanel left = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(12, 0));
        left.setPreferredSize(new Dimension(275, 58));
        left.add(nowArt, BorderLayout.WEST);
        JPanel names = Ui.column();
        names.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(7));
        names.add(nowTitle);
        names.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(5));
        names.add(nowArtist);
        left.add(names, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        footer.add(left, BorderLayout.WEST);
        JPanel middle = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(8, 0));
        middle.add(
                Ui.row(
                        iconButton("previous", 18, Ui.MUTED, () -> skip(-1)),
                        playButton,
                        iconButton("next", 18, Ui.MUTED, () -> skip(1))),
                BorderLayout.WEST);
        JPanel seek = Ui.column();
        JPanel labels = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
        labels.add(playMode, BorderLayout.WEST);
        labels.add(time, BorderLayout.EAST);
        seek.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(8));
        seek.add(labels);
        seek.add(progress);
        middle.add(seek, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        footer.add(middle, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        JSlider volume = new JSlider(0, 100, 65);
        volume.setPreferredSize(new Dimension(85, 25));
        volume.setToolTipText("음량");
        volume.addChangeListener(e -> player.volume(volume.getValue() / 100f));
        JPanel right = Ui.row(new JLabel(new MuseIcon("volume", 19, Ui.MUTED)), volume, planButton);
        right.setPreferredSize(new Dimension(265, 55));
        footer.add(right, BorderLayout.EAST);
        progress.setToolTipText("재생 위치 · 무료 이용은 1분까지 이동할 수 있습니다.");
        progress.addChangeListener(
                e -> {
                    if (!updatingSlider && !progress.getValueIsAdjusting())
                        player.seek(progress.getValue());
                });
        return footer;
    }

    private JButton iconButton(String name, int size, Color color, Runnable action) {
        JButton b = Ui.quiet("", action);
        b.setIcon(new MuseIcon(name, size, color));
        b.setMargin(new Insets(8, 7, 8, 7));
        b.setToolTipText(
                switch (name) {
                    case "play" -> "재생 / 일시정지";
                    case "next" -> "다음 곡";
                    case "previous" -> "이전 곡";
                    default -> name;
                });
        return b;
    }

    private void navigate(String next) {
        page = next;
        filter = "전체";
        search.setText("");
        render();
    }

    public void refresh() {
        if (loading) return;
        loading = true;
        connection.setText("음악 보관함을 불러오고 있습니다.");
        Member current = member;
        new SwingWorker<Loaded, Void>() {
            protected Loaded doInBackground() throws Exception {
                Member resolved = current == null ? new LocalProfileService().resolve() : current;
                PlaylistService ps = new PlaylistService(resolved);
                MusicLibraryService ms = new MusicLibraryService(resolved);
                MembershipService membershipService = new MembershipService(resolved);
                List<Playlist> publicLists =
                        ps.discover("").stream()
                                .filter(
                                        p ->
                                                p.owner().startsWith("local_")
                                                        || p.owner().equals(resolved.id()))
                                .toList();
                return new Loaded(
                        resolved,
                        ps,
                        ms,
                        membershipService,
                        ms.catalog(),
                        ps.mine(),
                        publicLists,
                        membershipService.status());
            }

            protected void done() {
                loading = false;
                if (!isDisplayable()) return;
                try {
                    Loaded d = get();
                    member = d.member();
                    playlists = d.playlists();
                    library = d.library();
                    membership = d.membership();
                    tracks = d.tracks();
                    mine = d.mine();
                    shared = d.shared();
                    pass = d.pass();
                    player.updatePass(pass);
                    connection.setText(
                            "연결됨 · 차트 정보 " + tracks.size() + "곡 · 나의 플레이리스트 " + mine.size() + "개");
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    library = null;
                    playlists = null;
                    membership = null;
                    tracks = List.of();
                    mine = List.of();
                    shared = List.of();
                    pass = PassStatus.free();
                    player.stop();
                    connection.setText("음악 보관함 연결이 필요합니다. 연결 설정에서 Oracle 접속 정보를 확인해 주세요.");
                }
                render();
            }
        }.execute();
    }

    private record Loaded(
            Member member,
            PlaylistService playlists,
            MusicLibraryService library,
            MembershipService membership,
            List<MediaTrack> tracks,
            List<Playlist> mine,
            List<Playlist> shared,
            PassStatus pass) {}

    private void render() {
        for (var entry : navigation.entrySet()) {
            boolean on = entry.getKey().equals(page);
            entry.getValue()
                    .putClientProperty(
                            "FlatLaf.style",
                            "background:"
                                    + (on ? "#FCE7EC" : "#F7F7F9")
                                    + "; foreground:"
                                    + (on ? "#D73250" : "#757681")
                                    + "; borderWidth:0; arc:12");
        }
        sectionTitle.setText(page);
        content.removeAll();
        JPanel body = Ui.column();
        body.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(24, 32, 26, 32));
        JPanel banner = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
        banner.setAlignmentX(Component.LEFT_ALIGNMENT);
        banner.add(connection, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        banner.setMaximumSize(new Dimension(Integer.MAX_VALUE, 28));
        body.add(banner);
        body.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(17));
        if (page.equals("홈")) renderHome(body);
        else if (page.equals("전체 곡")) renderSongs(body);
        else renderPlaylists(body);
        content.add(Ui.scroll(body), BorderLayout.CENTER);
        content.revalidate();
        content.repaint();
        updatePassLabel();
    }

    private void append(JPanel body, JComponent component) {
        component.setAlignmentX(Component.LEFT_ALIGNMENT);
        body.add(component);
    }

    private void renderHome(JPanel body) {
        append(body, Ui.text("오늘, 어떤 음악을 들을까요?", 27, true));
        body.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(8));
        append(body, Ui.muted("익숙한 명곡부터 새로운 취향까지, 음악이 머무는 곳."));
        body.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(23));
        JPanel hero =
                new JPanel(new BorderLayout()) {
                    protected void paintComponent(Graphics graphics) {
                        Graphics2D g = (Graphics2D) graphics.create();
                        g.setRenderingHint(
                                RenderingHints.KEY_ANTIALIASING, RenderingHints.VALUE_ANTIALIAS_ON);
                        g.setPaint(
                                new GradientPaint(
                                        0,
                                        0,
                                        new Color(31, 54, 58),
                                        getWidth(),
                                        getHeight(),
                                        new Color(75, 106, 96)));
                        g.fillRoundRect(0, 0, getWidth(), getHeight(), 22, 22);
                        g.setColor(new Color(223, 223, 178, 40));
                        for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++)
                            g.drawOval(
                                    getWidth() - 260 - i * 18,
                                    -50 - i * 18,
                                    280 + i * 36,
                                    280 + i * 36);
                        g.dispose();
                    }
                };
        hero.setOpaque(false);
        hero.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(21, 26, 20, 26));
        hero.setPreferredSize(new Dimension(800, 166));
        hero.setMaximumSize(new Dimension(Integer.MAX_VALUE, 166));
        JPanel heroText = Ui.column();
        heroText.setOpaque(false);
        JLabel eyebrow = Ui.text("MELON TOP100 · DATABASE LIBRARY", 10, true);
        eyebrow.setForeground(new Color(205, 221, 209));
        heroText.add(eyebrow);
        heroText.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(9));
        JLabel title = Ui.text("지금 가장 많이 듣는 음악", 26, true);
        title.setForeground(Color.WHITE);
        heroText.add(title);
        heroText.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(10));
        JLabel subtitle =
                Ui.text(
                        tracks.isEmpty()
                                ? "멜론 TOP100을 나만의 플레이리스트로."
                                : tracks.get(0).chartAt() + " 기준 · Oracle에 저장된 TOP100",
                        12,
                        false);
        subtitle.setForeground(new Color(221, 229, 222));
        heroText.add(subtitle);
        heroText.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(12));
        JButton listen = Ui.button("음악 둘러보기  →", () -> navigate("전체 곡"));
        listen.putClientProperty(
                "FlatLaf.style",
                "background:#FFFFFF;foreground:#29473D;arc:999;borderWidth:0;margin:5,14,5,14");
        heroText.add(listen);
        hero.add(heroText, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        JLabel disc =
                new JLabel(
                        tracks.isEmpty() ? new MusicArt(0, 120) : new AlbumArt(tracks.get(0), 120));
        disc.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(0, 18, 0, 10));
        hero.add(disc, BorderLayout.EAST);
        append(body, hero);
        body.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(24));
        append(body, section("TOP 4 · 지금 인기 있는 곡", Ui.quiet("전체 보기  →", () -> navigate("전체 곡"))));
        body.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(13));
        if (tracks.isEmpty()) append(body, empty("음악 보관함을 연결해 주세요", "연결하면 실제 곡과 앨범이 이곳에 표시됩니다."));
        else {
            JPanel cards = new JPanel(new GridLayout(1, 4, 18, 0));
            cards.setMaximumSize(new Dimension(Integer.MAX_VALUE, 255));
            cards.setPreferredSize(new Dimension(800, 255));
            for (MediaTrack t : tracks.stream().limit(4).toList()) cards.add(trackCard(t));
            append(body, cards);
        }
        body.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(23));
        append(body, section("멜론 TOP100", Ui.quiet("선택한 곡 담기", () -> addSelected())));

    private JPanel section(String title, JButton action) {
        JPanel p = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
        p.setMaximumSize(new Dimension(Integer.MAX_VALUE, 38));
        p.add(Ui.text(title, 19, true), BorderLayout.WEST);
        p.add(action, BorderLayout.EAST);
        return p;
    }

    private JPanel trackCard(MediaTrack track) {
        JPanel card = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(0, 9));
        JLabel cover = new JLabel(new AlbumArt(track, 175));
        card.add(cover, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        JPanel words = Ui.column();
        JLabel title = Ui.text(track.song().title(), 12, true);
        title.setToolTipText(track.song().title());
        words.add(title);
        words.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(5));
        words.add(Ui.muted(track.song().artists()));
        card.add(words, BorderLayout.SOUTH);
        MouseAdapter click =
                new MouseAdapter() {
                    public void mouseClicked(MouseEvent e) {
                        select(track);
                        play(track);
                    }
                };
        cover.setCursor(Cursor.getPredefinedCursor(Cursor.HAND_CURSOR));
        cover.addMouseListener(click);
        card.setToolTipText("곡 정보 / 재생 · " + track.song().title());
        return card;
    }

    private void renderSongs(JPanel body) {
        append(body, Ui.text("멜론 TOP100", 29, true));
        body.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(9));
        append(body, Ui.muted("멜론 차트에서 수집한 100곡 · 곡 정보 저장 / 보유 음원 연결"));
        body.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(22));
        JPanel pills = Ui.row();
        pills.setMaximumSize(new Dimension(Integer.MAX_VALUE, 50));
        for (String category : new String[] {"전체", "TOP 10", "TOP 50", "음원 연결됨"}) {
            JButton b =
                    Ui.button(
                            category,
                            () -> {
                                filter = category;
                                render();
                            });
            b.putClientProperty("FlatLaf.styleClass", category.equals(filter) ? "primary" : "pill");
            pills.add(b);
        }
        append(body, pills);
        body.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(14));
        String q = search.getText().strip().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        shownTracks =
                tracks.stream()
                        .filter(
                                t ->
                                        switch (filter) {
                                            case "TOP 10" -> t.rank() <= 10;
                                            case "TOP 50" -> t.rank() <= 50;
                                            case "음원 연결됨" -> t.playable();
                                            default -> true;
                                        })
                        .filter(
                                t ->
                                        (t.song().title()
                                                        + " "
                                                        + t.song().artists()
                                                        + " "
                                                        + t.song().album())
                                                .toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)
                                                .contains(q))
                        .toList();
        append(
                body,
                section("모든 곡  ·  " + shownTracks.size(), Ui.quiet("선택한 곡 담기", this::addSelected)));
        body.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(10));
        append(body, songTable(shownTracks));
        if (shownTracks.isEmpty())
            append(
                    body,
                    empty(
                            tracks.isEmpty() ? "연결된 음악이 없습니다" : "검색 결과가 없습니다",
                            tracks.isEmpty()
                                    ? "상단 연결 설정에서 보관함을 연결해 주세요."
                                    : "다른 제목이나 가수 이름으로 검색해 보세요."));
    }

    private JComponent songTable(List<MediaTrack> rows) {
        DefaultTableModel model = Ui.model("순위", "곡 / 가수", "앨범", "재생", "");
        for (int i = 0; i < rows.size(); i++) {
            Song s = rows.get(i).song();
            model.addRow(
                    new Object[] {
                        rows.get(i).rank(),
                        s.title(),
                        s.album(),
                        rows.get(i).playable() ? s.duration() : "정보",
                        "+"
                    });
        }
        JTable table = Ui.table(model);
        table.setRowHeight(58);
        table.getColumnModel().getColumn(0).setMaxWidth(42);
        table.getColumnModel().getColumn(3).setMaxWidth(67);
        table.getColumnModel().getColumn(4).setMaxWidth(42);
        table.getColumnModel().getColumn(1).setPreferredWidth(340);
        table.getColumnModel().getColumn(2).setPreferredWidth(240);
        table.getColumnModel()
                .getColumn(1)
                .setCellRenderer(
                        (t, value, isSelected, focus, row, column) -> {
                            MediaTrack track = rows.get(row);
                            JPanel panel = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(11, 0));
                            panel.setBackground(
                                    isSelected ? new Color(255, 240, 243) : Color.WHITE);
                            panel.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(5, 0, 5, 0));
                            panel.add(new JLabel(new AlbumArt(track, 40)), BorderLayout.WEST);
                            JPanel text = Ui.column();
                            text.setOpaque(false);
                            text.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(3));
                            text.add(Ui.text(track.song().title(), 12, true));
                            text.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(4));
                            text.add(Ui.muted(track.song().artists()));
                            panel.add(text, BorderLayout.CENTER);
                            return panel;
                        });
        table.getSelectionModel()
                .addListSelectionListener(
                        e -> {
                            int row = table.getSelectedRow();
                            if (row >= 0) select(rows.get(row));
                        });
        table.addMouseListener(
                new MouseAdapter() {
                    public void mouseClicked(MouseEvent e) {
                        int row = table.rowAtPoint(e.getPoint());
                        if (row < 0) return;
                        select(rows.get(row));
                        if (table.columnAtPoint(e.getPoint()) == 4) addSelected();
                        else if (e.getClickCount() == 2) play(rows.get(row));
                    }
                });
        table.getInputMap().put(KeyStroke.getKeyStroke("ENTER"), "listen");
        table.getActionMap()
                .put(
                        "listen",
                        new AbstractAction() {
                            public void actionPerformed(ActionEvent e) {
                                if (selected != null) play(selected);
                            }
                        });
        JScrollPane scroll = Ui.scroll(table);
        int height = 36 + Math.max(1, rows.size()) * 58;
        scroll.setPreferredSize(new Dimension(780, Math.min(430, height)));
        scroll.setMaximumSize(new Dimension(Integer.MAX_VALUE, Math.min(430, height)));
        return scroll;
    }

    private void renderPlaylists(JPanel body) {
        append(body, section(page, Ui.primary("+ 새 플레이리스트", this::create)));
        body.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(10));
        append(
                body,
                Ui.muted(
                        page.equals("내 플레이리스트")
                                ? "내 취향으로 채운 음악들을 한곳에."
                                : "다른 사람의 취향을 발견하고 좋아요로 저장하세요."));
        body.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(25));
        List<Playlist> rows =
                page.equals("내 플레이리스트")
                        ? mine
                        : shared.stream()
                                .filter(p -> !page.equals("좋아요한 목록") || p.liked())
                                .toList();
        if (rows.isEmpty()) {
            append(
                    body,
                    empty(
                            "아직 플레이리스트가 없어요",
                            page.equals("내 플레이리스트")
                                    ? "새 플레이리스트를 만들고 좋아하는 음악을 담아보세요."
                                    : "공개한 플레이리스트가 이곳에 모입니다."));
            return;
        }
        JPanel grid = new JPanel(new GridLayout(0, 3, 20, 20));
        for (Playlist p : rows) {
            JPanel card = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(0, 10));
            MusicArt art = new MusicArt(p.id(), 190);
            card.add(art, BorderLayout.CENTER);
            JPanel words = Ui.column();
            words.add(Ui.text(p.title(), 15, true));
            words.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(7));
            words.add(
                    Ui.muted(
                            p.nickname()
                                    + " · "
                                    + (p.isPublic() ? "공개" : "비공개")
                                    + " · 좋아요 "
                                    + p.likes()));
            words.add(
                    Ui.row(
                            Ui.quiet("열기", () -> open(p)),
                            Ui.quiet(p.liked() ? "좋아요 취소" : "좋아요", () -> like(p))));
            if (member != null && p.owner().equals(member.id()))
                words.add(Ui.row(Ui.quiet("수정", () -> edit(p)), Ui.quiet("삭제", () -> delete(p))));
            card.add(words, BorderLayout.SOUTH);
            grid.add(card);
            art.setCursor(Cursor.getPredefinedCursor(Cursor.HAND_CURSOR));
            art.addMouseListener(
                    new MouseAdapter() {
                        public void mouseClicked(MouseEvent e) {
                            open(p);
                        }
                    });
        }
        int h = ((rows.size() + 2) / 3) * 335;
        grid.setPreferredSize(new Dimension(780, h));
        grid.setMaximumSize(new Dimension(Integer.MAX_VALUE, h));
        append(body, grid);
    }

    private JPanel empty(String title, String subtitle) {
        JPanel p = Ui.column();
        p.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(30, 20, 30, 20));
        p.add(Ui.text(title, 17, true));
        p.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(9));
        p.add(Ui.muted(subtitle));
        p.setPreferredSize(new Dimension(700, 120));
        p.setMaximumSize(new Dimension(Integer.MAX_VALUE, 135));
        return p;
    }

    private boolean requireConnection() {
        if (playlists == null || library == null || membership == null) {
            ConnectionDialog.open(this, this::refresh);
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    private void select(MediaTrack track) {
        selected = track;
        if (!player.playing()) {
            nowTitle.setText(track.song().title());
            nowArtist.setText(track.song().artists());
            nowArt.setIcon(new AlbumArt(track, 48));
            time.setText("0:00 / " + track.song().duration());
        }
    }

    private void play(MediaTrack track) {
        if (!requireConnection()) return;
        if (!track.playable()) {
            int choice =
                    JOptionPane.showOptionDialog(
                            this,
                            "‘"
                                    + track.song().title()
                                    + "’ 곡 정보가 저장되어 있습니다.\n재생하려면 이 곡의 사용 권한이 있는 MP3 파일을 연결해 주세요.",
                            "곡 정보 · " + track.song().artists(),
                            JOptionPane.DEFAULT_OPTION,
                            JOptionPane.INFORMATION_MESSAGE,
                            null,
                            new String[] {"MP3 음원 연결", "멜론에서 곡 보기", "닫기"},
                            "닫기");
            if (choice == 0) attachAudio(track);
            else if (choice == 1) {
                try {
                    Desktop.getDesktop()
                            .browse(
                                    java.net.URI.create(
                                            "https://www.melon.com/song/detail.htm?songId="
                                                    + (track.song().id() - 1_000_000_000L)));
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    Ui.info(this, "웹 브라우저에서 멜론 곡 정보를 열 수 없습니다.");
                }
            }
            return;
        }
        Ui.work(
                this,
                () -> library.authorize(track.song().id()),
                authorized -> {
                    selected = authorized.track();
                    pass = authorized.pass();
                    player.play(selected, pass);
                    limitAnnounced = false;
                    nowTitle.setText(selected.song().title());
                    nowArtist.setText(selected.song().artists());
                    nowArt.setIcon(new AlbumArt(track, 48));
                    updatePassLabel();
                });
    }

    private void attachAudio(MediaTrack track) {
        JFileChooser chooser = new JFileChooser();
        chooser.setDialogTitle("선택한 곡의 보유 MP3 음원 연결");
        chooser.setFileFilter(new javax.swing.filechooser.FileNameExtensionFilter("MP3 음원", "mp3"));
        if (chooser.showOpenDialog(this) != JFileChooser.APPROVE_OPTION) return;
        java.nio.file.Path file = chooser.getSelectedFile().toPath();
        Ui.work(
                this,
                () -> {
                    new LocalAudioService().attach(track.song().id(), file);
                    return true;
                },
                ok -> {
                    refresh();
                    Ui.info(this, "음원을 연결했습니다. 이 곡을 다시 선택하면 재생됩니다.");
                });
    }

    private void updateChart() {
        if (!requireConnection()) return;
        Ui.work(
                this,
                () -> {
                    MelonChartService service = new MelonChartService();
                    var snapshot = service.fetch();
                    service.save(snapshot);
                    return snapshot;
                },
                snapshot -> {
                    refresh();
                    Ui.info(this, snapshot.chartAt() + " 기준 TOP100 100곡을 DB에 저장했습니다.");
                });
    }

    public void playSong(Song song) {
        tracks.stream()
                .filter(t -> t.song().id() == song.id())
                .findFirst()
                .ifPresentOrElse(
                        this::play, () -> Ui.info(this, "이 곡에는 재생 가능한 실제 음원이 연결되어 있지 않습니다."));
    }

    private void skip(int direction) {
        if (tracks.isEmpty()) return;
        int index = selected == null ? 0 : tracks.indexOf(selected);
        play(tracks.get(Math.floorMod(index + direction, tracks.size())));
    }

    private void tick() {
        playButton.setIcon(
                new MuseIcon(
                        player.playing() && !player.paused() ? "pause" : "play", 24, Ui.ACCENT));
        MediaTrack current = player.track();
        if (current != null) {
            if (!progress.getValueIsAdjusting()) {
                updatingSlider = true;
                progress.setMaximum(player.limit());
                progress.setValue((int) player.position());
                updatingSlider = false;
            }
            time.setText(format(player.position()) + " / " + current.song().duration());
        }
        if (player.limited() && !limitAnnounced) {
            limitAnnounced = true;
            playMode.setText("1분 미리듣기가 끝났어요 · Plus로 전체 듣기");
        }
        if (player.error() != null) playMode.setText(player.error());
    }

    private String format(double seconds) {
        int s = (int) seconds;
        return (s / 60) + ":" + String.format("%02d", s % 60);
    }

    private void updatePassLabel() {
        playMode.setText(pass.active() ? "Plus · 전체 듣기" : "무료 · 곡당 1분 미리듣기");
        planButton.setText(pass.active() ? "Plus 이용 중" : "Plus 이용권");
    }

    private void openMembership() {
        if (!requireConnection()) return;
        new MembershipDialog(
                        this,
                        membership,
                        () -> {
                            player.stop();
                            refresh();
                        })
                .setVisible(true);
    }

    private void create() {
        if (!requireConnection()) return;
        PlaylistEditor.open(
                this,
                null,
                v ->
                        Ui.work(
                                this,
                                () -> playlists.create(v.title(), v.description(), v.isPublic()),
                                id -> {
                                    page = "내 플레이리스트";
                                    refresh();
                                }));
    }

    private void open(Playlist p) {
        if (!requireConnection()) return;
        new DetailDialog(this, playlists, p.id(), this::refresh, this::playSong).setVisible(true);
    }

    private void edit(Playlist p) {
        if (!requireConnection()) return;
        PlaylistEditor.open(
                this,
                p,
                v ->
                        Ui.work(
                                this,
                                () -> {
                                    playlists.edit(
                                            p.id(), v.title(), v.description(), v.isPublic());
                                    return true;
                                },
                                ok -> refresh()));
    }

    private void delete(Playlist p) {
        if (!requireConnection()) return;
        if (JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
                        this,
                        "‘" + p.title() + "’ 플레이리스트를 삭제할까요? 곡 원본은 유지됩니다.",
                        "플레이리스트 삭제",
                        JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION)
                != JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) return;
        Ui.work(
                this,
                () -> {
                    playlists.delete(p.id());
                    return true;
                },
                ok -> refresh());
    }

    private void like(Playlist p) {
        if (!requireConnection()) return;
        Ui.work(
                this,
                () -> {
                    playlists.like(p.id(), !p.liked());
                    return true;
                },
                ok -> refresh());
    }

    private void addSelected() {
        if (selected == null) {
            Ui.info(this, "먼저 담을 곡을 선택해 주세요.");
            return;
        }
        if (!requireConnection()) return;
        if (mine.isEmpty()) {
            Ui.info(this, "곡을 담을 플레이리스트를 먼저 만들어 주세요.");
            create();
            return;
        }
        String[] names =
                mine.stream().map(p -> p.title() + "  (#" + p.id() + ")").toArray(String[]::new);
        String chosen =
                (String)
                        JOptionPane.showInputDialog(
                                this,
                                "곡을 담을 플레이리스트를 선택하세요.",
                                "플레이리스트에 담기",
                                JOptionPane.PLAIN_MESSAGE,
                                null,
                                names,
                                names[0]);
        if (chosen == null) return;
        int index = Arrays.asList(names).indexOf(chosen);
        long playlist = mine.get(index).id(), song = selected.song().id();
        Ui.work(
                this,
                () -> {
                    playlists.add(playlist, song);
                    return true;
                },
                ok -> {
                    Ui.info(this, "플레이리스트에 곡을 담았습니다.");
                    refresh();
                });
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/ui/MembershipDialog.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.ui;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.*;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service.MembershipService;

import java.awt.*;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.UUID;

import javax.swing.*;
import javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel;

public final class MembershipDialog extends JDialog {
    private final MembershipService service;
    private final Runnable changed;
    private final JLabel status = Ui.text("이용권 확인 중", 18, true);
    private final JButton buy, cancel;
    private final DefaultTableModel history = Ui.model("결제일", "모의 금액", "사용 기한", "상태");
    private String requestId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();

    public MembershipDialog(Window owner, MembershipService service, Runnable changed) {
        super(owner, "muse plus · 이용권", ModalityType.APPLICATION_MODAL);
        this.service = service;
        this.changed = changed;
        JPanel p = Ui.padded(30), top = Ui.column();
        JLabel brand = Ui.text("muse plus", 32, true);
        brand.setForeground(Ui.ACCENT);
        top.add(brand);
        top.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(10));
        top.add(Ui.text("좋아하는 음악을 끝까지.", 23, true));
        top.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(12));
        top.add(Ui.muted("연결한 음원: 무료 1분  ·  Plus 이용권: 전체 길이 재생"));
        top.add(Ui.muted("멜론 음원 스트리밍 권한은 포함되지 않습니다."));
        top.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(22));
        top.add(Ui.text("30일 이용권   ₩4,900", 22, true));
        top.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(8));
        top.add(Ui.muted("과제용 모의 결제입니다. 실제 청구 금액은 0원입니다."));
        top.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(20));
        top.add(status);
        top.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(12));
        buy =
                Ui.primary(
                        "모의 결제하고 전체 듣기",
                        () -> {
                            Ui.work(
                                    this,
                                    () -> {
                                        service.purchase(requestId);
                                        return true;
                                    },
                                    ok -> {
                                        requestId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
                                        changed.run();
                                        load();
                                    });
                        });
        cancel =
                Ui.quiet(
                        "최근 모의 결제 취소",
                        () -> {
                            if (JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
                                            this,
                                            "최근 모의 결제를 취소하고 이용권 상태를 갱신할까요?",
                                            "모의 결제 취소",
                                            JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION)
                                    == JOptionPane.YES_OPTION)
                                Ui.work(
                                        this,
                                        () -> {
                                            service.cancelLatest();
                                            return true;
                                        },
                                        ok -> {
                                            changed.run();
                                            load();
                                        });
                        });
        top.add(Ui.row(buy, cancel));
        p.add(top, BorderLayout.NORTH);
        JTable table = Ui.table(history);
        table.setRowHeight(38);
        JScrollPane scroll = Ui.scroll(table);
        scroll.setPreferredSize(new Dimension(600, 155));
        p.add(scroll, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        p.add(Ui.row(Ui.quiet("닫기", this::dispose)), BorderLayout.SOUTH);
        setContentPane(p);
        setSize(700, 670);
        setMinimumSize(new Dimension(640, 600));
        setLocationRelativeTo(owner);
        SwingUtilities.invokeLater(this::load);
    }

    private record Data(PassStatus pass, java.util.List<PaymentOrder> orders) {}

    private void load() {
        Ui.work(
                this,
                () -> new Data(service.status(), service.history()),
                data -> {
                    boolean active = data.pass().active();
                    DateTimeFormatter f = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy.MM.dd HH:mm");
                    status.setText(
                            active
                                    ? "Plus 이용 중 · " + data.pass().expiresAt().format(f) + "까지"
                                    : "무료 이용 중 · 곡당 1분 미리듣기");
                    buy.setEnabled(!active);
                    cancel.setEnabled(
                            data.orders().stream().anyMatch(o -> o.status().equals("PAID")));
                    history.setRowCount(0);
                    for (PaymentOrder o : data.orders())
                        history.addRow(
                                new Object[] {
                                    o.paidAt().format(f),
                                    "₩" + o.amount(),
                                    o.validUntil().format(f),
                                    o.status().equals("PAID") ? "모의 결제 완료" : "모의 결제 취소"
                                });
                });
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/ui/MuseIcon.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.ui;

import java.awt.*;
import java.awt.geom.*;

import javax.swing.*;

public final class MuseIcon implements Icon {
    private final String name;
    private final int size;
    private final Color color;

    public MuseIcon(String name, int size, Color color) {
        this.name = name;
        this.size = size;
        this.color = color;
    }

    public int getIconWidth() {
        return size;
    }

    public int getIconHeight() {
        return size;
    }

    public void paintIcon(Component c, Graphics graphics, int x, int y) {
        Graphics2D g = (Graphics2D) graphics.create();
        g.translate(x, y);
        g.scale(size / 24.0, size / 24.0);
        g.setColor(color);
        g.setStroke(new BasicStroke(1.7f, BasicStroke.CAP_ROUND, BasicStroke.JOIN_ROUND));
        switch (name) {
            case "play" -> {
                Path2D p = new Path2D.Float();
                p.moveTo(8, 5);
                p.lineTo(20, 12);
                p.lineTo(8, 19);
                p.closePath();
                g.fill(p);
            }
            case "pause" -> {
                g.fillRoundRect(7, 5, 4, 14, 1, 1);
                g.fillRoundRect(14, 5, 4, 14, 1, 1);
            }
            case "next" -> {
                Path2D p = new Path2D.Float();
                p.moveTo(5, 5);
                p.lineTo(16, 12);
                p.lineTo(5, 19);
                p.closePath();
                g.fill(p);
                g.drawLine(19, 5, 19, 19);
            }
            case "previous" -> {
                Path2D p = new Path2D.Float();
                p.moveTo(19, 5);
                p.lineTo(8, 12);
                p.lineTo(19, 19);
                p.closePath();
                g.fill(p);
                g.drawLine(5, 5, 5, 19);
            }
            case "home" -> {
                Path2D p = new Path2D.Float();
                p.moveTo(3, 10);
                p.lineTo(12, 3);
                p.lineTo(21, 10);
                p.moveTo(6, 9);
                p.lineTo(6, 21);
                p.lineTo(18, 21);
                p.lineTo(18, 9);
                p.moveTo(10, 21);
                p.lineTo(10, 14);
                p.lineTo(14, 14);
                p.lineTo(14, 21);
                g.draw(p);
            }
            case "heart" -> {
                Path2D p = new Path2D.Float();
                p.moveTo(12, 21);
                p.curveTo(-8, 8, 7, -3, 12, 7);
                p.curveTo(17, -3, 32, 8, 12, 21);
                g.draw(p);
            }
            case "search" -> {
                g.drawOval(3, 3, 13, 13);
                g.drawLine(16, 16, 22, 22);
            }
            case "plus" -> {
                g.drawLine(12, 4, 12, 20);
                g.drawLine(4, 12, 20, 12);
            }
            case "music" -> {
                g.drawLine(9, 17, 9, 5);
                g.drawLine(9, 5, 20, 3);
                g.drawLine(20, 3, 20, 15);
                g.drawOval(3, 16, 6, 5);
                g.drawOval(14, 14, 6, 5);
            }
            case "volume" -> {
                g.drawRect(3, 9, 4, 6);
                Path2D p = new Path2D.Float();
                p.moveTo(7, 9);
                p.lineTo(12, 5);
                p.lineTo(12, 19);
                p.lineTo(7, 15);
                g.draw(p);
                g.draw(new Arc2D.Float(10, 4, 12, 16, -65, 130, Arc2D.OPEN));
            }
            case "library" -> {
                g.drawRoundRect(3, 4, 5, 17, 2, 2);
                g.drawRoundRect(10, 4, 5, 17, 2, 2);
                g.drawLine(18, 4, 22, 20);
            }
            default -> {
                g.drawRoundRect(4, 4, 16, 16, 4, 4);
                g.drawLine(8, 9, 16, 9);
                g.drawLine(8, 15, 16, 15);
            }
        }
        g.dispose();
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/ui/MusicArt.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.ui;

import java.awt.*;
import java.awt.geom.*;

import javax.swing.*;

/** 외부 앨범 표지를 복제하지 않는 코드 기반 커버 그래픽. */
public final class MusicArt extends JComponent implements Icon {
    private final int variant, size;
    private static final Color[][] PALETTES = {
        {new Color(36, 62, 75), new Color(124, 168, 172)},
        {new Color(205, 108, 84), new Color(245, 203, 157)},
        {new Color(107, 101, 161), new Color(214, 195, 231)},
        {new Color(69, 116, 98), new Color(190, 211, 165)}
    };

    public MusicArt(long key, int size) {
        variant = Math.floorMod((int) key - 9001, 4);
        this.size = size;
        setPreferredSize(new Dimension(size, size));
        setMinimumSize(new Dimension(48, 48));
    }

    @Override
    public int getIconWidth() {
        return size;
    }

    @Override
    public int getIconHeight() {
        return size;
    }

    @Override
    public void paintIcon(Component c, Graphics g, int x, int y) {
        Graphics2D copy = (Graphics2D) g.create();
        copy.translate(x, y);
        draw(copy, size, size);
        copy.dispose();
    }

    @Override
    protected void paintComponent(Graphics g) {
        draw((Graphics2D) g, getWidth(), getHeight());
    }

    private void draw(Graphics2D source, int width, int height) {
        Graphics2D g = (Graphics2D) source.create();
        g.setRenderingHint(RenderingHints.KEY_ANTIALIASING, RenderingHints.VALUE_ANTIALIAS_ON);
        g.clip(new RoundRectangle2D.Float(0, 0, width, height, 18, 18));
        Color[] colors = PALETTES[variant];
        g.setPaint(new GradientPaint(0, 0, colors[0], width, height, colors[1]));
        g.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
        double scale = Math.min(width, height) / 200.0;
        g.scale(width / 200.0, height / 200.0);
        g.setColor(new Color(255, 255, 255, 32));
        g.setStroke(new BasicStroke(1.2f));
        for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
            double r = 45 + i * 14;
            g.draw(new Ellipse2D.Double(105 - r, 70 - r, r * 2, r * 2));
        }
        g.setColor(new Color(255, 248, 229, 185));
        if (variant == 0) {
            g.fill(new Ellipse2D.Double(103, 33, 54, 54));
            g.setColor(colors[0]);
            g.fill(new Ellipse2D.Double(121, 26, 51, 51));
        } else if (variant == 1) {
            g.rotate(-.45, 110, 100);
            for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
                g.setColor(new Color(255, 244, 222, 45 + i * 22));
                g.fillRoundRect(30 + i * 24, 40, 12, 170, 10, 10);
            }
            g.rotate(.45, 110, 100);
        } else if (variant == 2) {
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
                g.fill(new Arc2D.Double(28 + i * 31, 35 + i * 18, 90, 90, 20, 270, Arc2D.OPEN));
        } else {
            g.fill(new Ellipse2D.Double(40, 35, 105, 105));
            g.setColor(new Color(46, 88, 76, 180));
            for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++) g.fillRoundRect(12, 105 + i * 8, 180, 3, 3, 3);
        }
        if (width > 95 && height > 95) {
            g.setColor(Color.WHITE);
            g.setFont(new Font("Segoe UI", Font.BOLD, 13));
            g.drawString(
                    new String[] {"NOCTURNE", "FANTAISIE", "WALTZ", "CHOPIN"}[variant], 18, 173);
            g.setFont(new Font("Segoe UI", Font.PLAIN, 8));
            g.drawString("THE PIANO COLLECTION", 18, 187);
        }
        g.dispose();
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/ui/PlaylistEditor.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.ui;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.Playlist;

import java.awt.*;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

import javax.swing.*;

public final class PlaylistEditor {
    public record Values(String title, String description, boolean isPublic) {}

    private PlaylistEditor() {}

    public static void open(Window owner, Playlist existing, Consumer<Values> submit) {
        JDialog d =
                new JDialog(
                        owner,
                        existing == null ? "플레이리스트 만들기" : "플레이리스트 수정",
                        Dialog.ModalityType.APPLICATION_MODAL);
        JPanel p = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(10, 18));
        p.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(24, 24, 24, 24));
        JTextField title = new JTextField(existing == null ? "" : existing.title(), 30);
        JTextArea description =
                new JTextArea(existing == null ? "" : Ui.safe(existing.description()), 6, 30);
        description.setLineWrap(true);
        description.setWrapStyleWord(true);
        JCheckBox pub = new JCheckBox("다른 회원에게 공개", existing != null && existing.isPublic());
        JPanel top = Ui.column();
        top.add(new JLabel("제목 (필수, 150자 이하)"));
        top.add(Box.createVerticalStrut(8));
        top.add(title);
        p.add(top, BorderLayout.NORTH);
        JPanel middle = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(0, 8));
        middle.add(new JLabel("설명 (1000자 이하)"), BorderLayout.NORTH);
        middle.add(new JScrollPane(description), BorderLayout.CENTER);
        p.add(middle, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        p.add(
                Ui.row(
                        pub,
                        Ui.button(
                                "저장",
                                () -> {
                                    Values v =
                                            new Values(
                                                    title.getText(),
                                                    description.getText(),
                                                    pub.isSelected());
                                    try {
                                        kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service.AuthService.text(
                                                v.title(), "제목", 150, true);
                                        kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service.AuthService.text(
                                                v.description(), "설명", 1000, false);
                                    } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                                        Ui.error(d, e);
                                        return;
                                    }
                                    d.dispose();
                                    submit.accept(v);
                                }),
                        Ui.button("취소", d::dispose)),
                BorderLayout.SOUTH);
        d.setContentPane(p);
        d.pack();
        d.setMinimumSize(new Dimension(540, 360));
        d.setLocationRelativeTo(owner);
        d.setVisible(true);
    }
}
```

## src/main/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/ui/Ui.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.ui;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service.AppException;

import java.awt.*;
import java.awt.event.*;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.concurrent.*;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

import javax.swing.*;
import javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel;

public final class Ui {
    private Ui() {}

    public static final Color INK = new Color(32, 33, 39),
            MUTED = new Color(128, 129, 141),
            ACCENT = new Color(226, 62, 88),
            SOFT = new Color(246, 246, 249);

    public static void theme() {
        try (java.io.InputStream in = Ui.class.getResourceAsStream("/theme/muse.properties")) {
            if (in == null) throw new java.io.IOException("테마 파일 누락");
            UIManager.setLookAndFeel(new com.formdev.flatlaf.FlatPropertiesLaf("Muse", in));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("화면 테마를 불러올 수 없습니다.", e);
        }
    }

    public static JLabel text(String text, int size, boolean bold) {
        JLabel l = new JLabel(text);
        l.setFont(new Font("맑은 고딕", bold ? Font.BOLD : Font.PLAIN, size));
        l.setForeground(INK);
        return l;
    }

    public static JLabel muted(String text) {
        JLabel l = text(text, 12, false);
        l.setForeground(MUTED);
        return l;
    }

    public static JButton primary(String text, Runnable action) {
        JButton b = button(text, action);
        b.putClientProperty("FlatLaf.styleClass", "primary");
        return b;
    }

    public static JButton quiet(String text, Runnable action) {
        JButton b = button(text, action);
        b.putClientProperty("FlatLaf.styleClass", "quiet");
        return b;
    }

    public static JPanel padded(int padding) {
        JPanel p = new JPanel(new BorderLayout());
        p.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(padding, padding, padding, padding));
        return p;
    }

    public static JScrollPane scroll(Component content) {
        JScrollPane s = new JScrollPane(content);
        s.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder());
        s.getVerticalScrollBar().setUnitIncrement(24);
        s.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(ScrollPaneConstants.HORIZONTAL_SCROLLBAR_NEVER);
        return s;
    }

    public static JPanel column() {
        JPanel p = new JPanel();
        p.setLayout(new BoxLayout(p, BoxLayout.Y_AXIS));
        return p;
    }

    public static JPanel row(Component... components) {
        JPanel p = new JPanel(new FlowLayout(FlowLayout.LEFT, 10, 8));
        p.setAlignmentX(Component.LEFT_ALIGNMENT);
        for (Component c : components) p.add(c);
        return p;
    }

    public static JButton button(String text, Runnable action) {
        JButton b = new JButton(text);
        b.setFocusPainted(false);
        b.addActionListener(e -> action.run());
        return b;
    }

    public static JLabel heading(String text) {
        JLabel l = new JLabel(text);
        l.setFont(l.getFont().deriveFont(Font.BOLD, 25f));
        return l;
    }

    public static DefaultTableModel model(String... headers) {
        return new DefaultTableModel(headers, 0) {
            @Override
            public boolean isCellEditable(int r, int c) {
                return false;
            }
        };
    }

    public static JTable table(DefaultTableModel model) {
        JTable t = new JTable(model);
        t.setRowHeight(58);
        t.setSelectionMode(ListSelectionModel.SINGLE_SELECTION);
        t.getTableHeader().setReorderingAllowed(false);
        t.setFillsViewportHeight(true);
        return t;
    }

    public static void info(Component owner, String message) {
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(owner, message, "안내", JOptionPane.INFORMATION_MESSAGE);
    }

    public static void error(Component owner, Throwable e) {
        while ((e instanceof ExecutionException || e instanceof CompletionException)
                && e.getCause() != null) e = e.getCause();
        String text;
        if (e instanceof AppException) text = e.getMessage();
        else if (e instanceof SQLException s) {
            text =
                    switch (s.getErrorCode()) {
                        case 1 -> "이미 등록된 항목입니다. 새로고침 후 다시 확인해 주세요.";
                        case 1017 -> "Oracle 사용자 이름 또는 비밀번호를 확인해 주세요.";
                        case 942, 2289 ->
                                "DB 테이블 또는 시퀀스가 없습니다. sql/02_add_sequence.sql과"
                                        + " sql/06_music_and_membership.sql을 실행해 주세요.";
                        case 54, 30006 -> "다른 작업이 목록을 변경 중입니다. 잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.";
                        case 2291 -> "연결할 회원 또는 곡이 없습니다. 새로고침해 주세요.";
                        default ->
                                s.getSQLState() == null
                                                && s.getErrorCode() == 0
                                                && s.getMessage() != null
                                                && s.getMessage().startsWith("DB 설정")
                                        ? s.getMessage()
                                        : s.getMessage() != null
                                                        && s.getMessage()
                                                                .startsWith("config/db.properties")
                                                ? s.getMessage()
                                                : "데이터베이스 작업을 완료하지 못했습니다. Oracle 실행 상태와 외부 설정을 확인해"
                                                        + " 주세요. (오류 번호: "
                                                        + s.getErrorCode()
                                                        + ")";
                    };
        } else text = "작업을 완료하지 못했습니다. 입력 내용을 확인하고 다시 시도해 주세요.";
        JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(owner, text, "처리 안내", JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
    }

    /** DB와 암호화는 작업 스레드, 화면 반영은 done()의 EDT에서 실행한다. */
    public static <T> void work(Component owner, Callable<T> job, Consumer<T> success) {
        JRootPane root = SwingUtilities.getRootPane(owner);
        if (root != null && Boolean.TRUE.equals(root.getClientProperty("busy"))) return;
        if (root != null) {
            root.putClientProperty("busy", true);
            JPanel glass = new JPanel();
            glass.setOpaque(false);
            glass.addMouseListener(new MouseAdapter() {});
            glass.setFocusTraversalKeysEnabled(false);
            glass.addKeyListener(new KeyAdapter() {});
            root.setGlassPane(glass);
            glass.setCursor(Cursor.getPredefinedCursor(Cursor.WAIT_CURSOR));
            glass.setVisible(true);
            glass.setFocusable(true);
            glass.requestFocusInWindow();
        }
        new SwingWorker<T, Void>() {
            @Override
            protected T doInBackground() throws Exception {
                return job.call();
            }

            @Override
            protected void done() {
                if (root != null) {
                    root.getGlassPane().setVisible(false);
                    root.putClientProperty("busy", false);
                }
                try {
                    T result = get();
                    if (owner.isDisplayable()) success.accept(result);
                } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                } catch (ExecutionException | RuntimeException e) {
                    if (owner.isDisplayable()) error(owner, e);
                }
            }
        }.execute();
    }

    public static String safe(String value) {
        return value == null ? "" : value;
    }
}
```
