## src/test/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/AudioDeviceCheck.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.audio.*;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.*;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

/** 수동 실행: 실제 기본 사운드 장치를 열되 음량 0으로 경계 프레임을 검증한다. */
public final class AudioDeviceCheck {
    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
        var track =
                new MediaTrack(
                        new Song(
                                9001,
                                "녹턴 2번",
                                "Frédéric Chopin",
                                "Nocturnes, Op. 9",
                                "클래식",
                                277,
                                0),
                        "audio/nocturne.mp3",
                        "Musopen",
                        "https://archive.org/details/musopen-chopin",
                        "CC0 1.0");
        try (AudioPlayer p = new AudioPlayer()) {
            p.volume(0);
            p.play(track, PassStatus.free());
            long until = System.currentTimeMillis() + 10000;
            while (p.position() < .1 && p.playing() && System.currentTimeMillis() < until)
                Thread.sleep(50);
            if (p.error() != null) throw new IllegalStateException(p.error());
            if (p.position() < .1) throw new IllegalStateException("사운드 장치 시작 시간 초과");
            p.seek(59);
            until = System.currentTimeMillis() + 10000;
            while (p.playing() && System.currentTimeMillis() < until) Thread.sleep(50);
            if (!p.limited() || p.position() > 60.01 || p.position() < 59.9)
                throw new IllegalStateException("무료 재생 경계 오류: " + p.position());
            LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
            p.play(track, new PassStatus(now.plusDays(30), now));
            Thread.sleep(300);
            p.seek(61);
            until = System.currentTimeMillis() + 10000;
            while (p.position() < 61.1 && p.playing() && System.currentTimeMillis() < until)
                Thread.sleep(50);
            if (p.position() < 61.1)
                throw new IllegalStateException("전체 재생 검증 실패: " + p.position());
            p.togglePause();
            Thread.sleep(250);
            double before = p.position();
            Thread.sleep(300);
            if (Math.abs(p.position() - before) > .15) throw new IllegalStateException("일시정지 실패");
            p.togglePause();
            Thread.sleep(300);
            System.out.println("실제 오디오 장치: 무료 60초 정지, 유료 61초 이후 재생, 일시정지·재개 확인 (음량 0)");
        }
    }
}
```

## src/test/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/CaptureScreens.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.audio.AudioPlayer;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.config.Database;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.dao.Jdbc;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.*;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service.*;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.ui.*;

import java.awt.*;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.nio.file.*;
import java.sql.*;
import java.util.concurrent.*;

import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import javax.swing.*;

/** 실제 Oracle 데이터로 실행하는 화면 캡처. 새 임시 계정의 데이터만 정리한다. */
public final class CaptureScreens {
    private static <T> T edt(Callable<T> task) throws Exception {
        FutureTask<T> f = new FutureTask<>(task);
        SwingUtilities.invokeAndWait(f);
        return f.get();
    }

    private static Object field(Object o, String name) throws Exception {
        Field f = o.getClass().getDeclaredField(name);
        f.setAccessible(true);
        return f.get(o);
    }

    private static void invoke(Object o, String name, Class<?>[] types, Object... args)
            throws Exception {
        Method m = o.getClass().getDeclaredMethod(name, types);
        m.setAccessible(true);
        m.invoke(o, args);
    }

    private static void settle(Window w) throws Exception {
        for (int i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
            Thread.sleep(100);
            boolean busy =
                    edt(
                            () -> {
                                if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(
                                        ((RootPaneContainer) w)
                                                .getRootPane()
                                                .getClientProperty("busy"))) return true;
                                return w instanceof MainFrame && (boolean) field(w, "loading");
                            });
            if (!busy) {
                edt(
                        () -> {
                            w.validate();
                            return null;
                        });
                return;
            }
        }
        throw new IllegalStateException("화면 로딩 시간 초과");
    }

    private static void capture(Window w, String name) throws Exception {
        settle(w);
        edt(
                () -> {
                    JComponent content = (JComponent) ((RootPaneContainer) w).getContentPane();
                    BufferedImage image =
                            new BufferedImage(
                                    content.getWidth(),
                                    content.getHeight(),
                                    BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_RGB);
                    Graphics2D g = image.createGraphics();
                    content.printAll(g);
                    g.dispose();
                    ImageIO.write(
                            image, "png", Path.of("docs/screenshots/" + name + ".png").toFile());
                    return null;
                });
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
        Files.createDirectories(Path.of("docs/screenshots"));
        Path temporary = Files.createTempDirectory(Path.of("target"), "capture-");
        Member member = new LocalProfileService().resolve(temporary.resolve("profile.properties"));
        PlaylistService service = new PlaylistService(member);
        MembershipService membership = new MembershipService(member);
        MusicLibraryService music = new MusicLibraryService(member);
        MainFrame main = null;
        long id = 0;
        var chart = music.catalog();
        long first = chart.get(0).song().id(),
                second = chart.get(1).song().id(),
                third = chart.get(2).song().id();
        try {
            main =
                    edt(
                            () -> {
                                Ui.theme();
                                MainFrame f = new MainFrame(member);
                                f.setVisible(true);
                                return f;
                            });
            MainFrame f = main;
            settle(f);
            if (edt(() -> field(f, "library")) == null)
                throw new IllegalStateException("실제 Oracle 보관함 연결 실패");
            edt(
                    () -> {
                        ((AudioPlayer) field(f, "player")).volume(0);
                        return null;
                    });
            Thread.sleep(3000);
            capture(f, "13_home_live");
            edt(
                    () -> {
                        invoke(f, "navigate", new Class<?>[] {String.class}, "전체 곡");
                        return null;
                    });
            capture(f, "14_library_live");
            id = service.create("오늘의 TOP100", "멜론 차트에서 발견한 나만의 음악.", true);
            service.add(id, first);
            service.add(id, second);
            service.add(id, third);
            service.like(id, true);
            edt(
                    () -> {
                        invoke(f, "navigate", new Class<?>[] {String.class}, "내 플레이리스트");
                        f.refresh();
                        return null;
                    });
            capture(f, "15_my_playlist_live");
            long playlist = id;
            DetailDialog detail =
                    edt(
                            () -> {
                                DetailDialog d =
                                        new DetailDialog(
                                                f, service, playlist, f::refresh, f::playSong);
                                d.setModalityType(Dialog.ModalityType.MODELESS);
                                d.setVisible(true);
                                return d;
                            });
            capture(detail, "16_playlist_live");
            service.move(id, third, -1);
            edt(
                    () -> {
                        detail.reload();
                        return null;
                    });
            capture(detail, "17_reorder_live");
            edt(
                    () -> {
                        detail.dispose();
                        return null;
                    });
            MembershipDialog free =
                    edt(
                            () -> {
                                MembershipDialog d =
                                        new MembershipDialog(f, membership, f::refresh);
                                d.setModalityType(Dialog.ModalityType.MODELESS);
                                d.setVisible(true);
                                return d;
                            });
            capture(free, "18_free_pass_live");
            edt(
                    () -> {
                        free.dispose();
                        return null;
                    });
            membership.purchase(java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString());
            MembershipDialog paid =
                    edt(
                            () -> {
                                MembershipDialog d =
                                        new MembershipDialog(f, membership, f::refresh);
                                d.setModalityType(Dialog.ModalityType.MODELESS);
                                d.setVisible(true);
                                return d;
                            });
            capture(paid, "19_paid_pass_live");
            edt(
                    () -> {
                        paid.dispose();
                        f.refresh();
                        return null;
                    });
            settle(f);
            edt(
                    () -> {
                        invoke(f, "navigate", new Class<?>[] {String.class}, "전체 곡");
                        invoke(f, "select", new Class<?>[] {MediaTrack.class}, chart.get(0));
                        return null;
                    });
            capture(f, "20_chart_selection_live");
            membership.cancelLatest();
            MembershipDialog cancelled =
                    edt(
                            () -> {
                                MembershipDialog d =
                                        new MembershipDialog(f, membership, f::refresh);
                                d.setModalityType(Dialog.ModalityType.MODELESS);
                                d.setVisible(true);
                                return d;
                            });
            capture(cancelled, "21_cancelled_pass_live");
            edt(
                    () -> {
                        cancelled.dispose();
                        return null;
                    });
            service.remove(id, second);
            service.delete(id);
            id = 0;
            edt(
                    () -> {
                        invoke(f, "navigate", new Class<?>[] {String.class}, "내 플레이리스트");
                        f.refresh();
                        return null;
                    });
            capture(f, "22_deleted_live");
            edt(
                    () -> {
                        f.dispose();
                        return null;
                    });
            System.out.println("실제 Oracle TOP100 화면 10개 캡처 완료 (음원 연결 전 차트 정보 표시)");
        } finally {
            if (main != null) {
                MainFrame f = main;
                edt(
                        () -> {
                            f.dispose();
                            return null;
                        });
            }
            try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
                if (id != 0)
                    Jdbc.update(
                            c,
                            "DELETE FROM PLAYLIST WHERE playlist_id=? AND member_id=?",
                            id,
                            member.id());
                Jdbc.update(c, "DELETE FROM MEMBERSHIP_ORDER WHERE member_id=?", member.id());
                Jdbc.update(c, "DELETE FROM MEMBER WHERE member_id=?", member.id());
            }
        }
        System.exit(0);
    }
}
```

## src/test/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/CoreTest.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.security.PasswordHasher;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service.*;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class CoreTest {
    @Test
    void passwordRoundTripAndSalt() {
        String a = PasswordHasher.hash("테스트 암호!".toCharArray()),
                b = PasswordHasher.hash("테스트 암호!".toCharArray());
        assertNotEquals(a, b);
        assertTrue(a.length() <= 255);
        assertTrue(PasswordHasher.verify("테스트 암호!".toCharArray(), a));
        assertFalse(PasswordHasher.verify("틀린 암호".toCharArray(), a));
    }

    @Test
    void demoAndMalformedHashesCannotAuthenticate() {
        for (String hash :
                new String[] {
                    "DEMO_HASH_NOT_FOR_AUTH_01",
                    "pbkdf2_sha256$-1$a$b",
                    "pbkdf2_sha256$99999999$a$b",
                    "garbage",
                    ""
                }) assertFalse(PasswordHasher.verify("anything".toCharArray(), hash));
    }

    @Test
    void oracleCharLimitsUseUnicodeCharacters() {
        assertEquals("가".repeat(30), AuthService.text("가".repeat(30), "아이디", 30, true));
        assertEquals("😀".repeat(30), AuthService.text("😀".repeat(30), "아이디", 30, true));
        assertThrows(AppException.class, () -> AuthService.text("가".repeat(31), "아이디", 30, true));
        assertThrows(AppException.class, () -> AuthService.text("   ", "제목", 150, true));
    }
}
```

## src/test/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/LocalAudioOracleTest.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.config.Database;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.dao.Jdbc;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service.*;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

import java.nio.file.*;
import java.sql.*;

@Tag("oracle")
class LocalAudioOracleTest {
    @TempDir Path directory;

    @Test
    void connectsOwnedMp3AndKeepsChartMetadataSeparate() throws Exception {
        long id = 987654321;
        String resource = null;
        var profile = new LocalProfileService().resolve(directory.resolve("profile.properties"));
        try {
            try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
                Jdbc.update(
                        c,
                        "INSERT INTO SONG(song_id,title,album_title) VALUES(?,'강아지 왈츠 음원 연결"
                            + " 검증','CC0 검증')",
                        id);
            }
            Path source = directory.resolve("waltz.mp3");
            try (var in = getClass().getResourceAsStream("/audio/minute-waltz.mp3")) {
                Files.copy(in, source);
            }
            new LocalAudioService().attach(id, source);
            var authorized = new MusicLibraryService(profile).authorize(id);
            resource = authorized.track().resource();
            assertTrue(resource.matches("local-audio/[a-f0-9-]+[.]mp3"));
            assertEquals(108, authorized.track().song().seconds());
            assertEquals(60, authorized.pass().limitSeconds(108));
            assertTrue(Files.exists(Path.of("config/audio", resource.substring(12))));
            assertThrows(
                    AppException.class,
                    () -> new MusicLibraryService(profile).authorize(1_602_290_711L));
        } finally {
            try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
                Jdbc.update(c, "DELETE FROM SONG_MEDIA WHERE song_id=?", id);
                Jdbc.update(c, "DELETE FROM SONG WHERE song_id=?", id);
                Jdbc.update(c, "DELETE FROM MEMBER WHERE member_id=?", profile.id());
            }
            if (resource != null)
                Files.deleteIfExists(Path.of("config/audio", resource.substring(12)));
        }
    }
}
```

## src/test/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/MelonChartTest.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service.*;

import org.jsoup.Jsoup;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

class MelonChartTest {
    private String html() throws Exception {
        try (var in = getClass().getResourceAsStream("/melon-top100.html")) {
            return new String(in.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void parsesRealSnapshotAndStableExternalIds() throws Exception {
        var snapshot = new MelonChartService().parse(Jsoup.parse(html(), MelonChartService.SOURCE));
        assertEquals(LocalDateTime.of(2026, 11, 26, 11, 0), snapshot.chartAt());
        assertEquals(100, snapshot.entries().size());
        assertEquals("꿈꾸던 어른이 되었나요?", snapshot.entries().get(0).title());
        assertEquals(1_602_290_711L, snapshot.entries().get(0).songId());
        assertEquals(2, snapshot.entries().get(2).artists().size());
        assertEquals(100, snapshot.entries().get(99).rank());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsPartialOrDuplicateRankWithoutTouchingDatabase() throws Exception {
        var partial = Jsoup.parse(html());
        partial.select("tr").last().remove();
        assertThrows(AppException.class, () -> new MelonChartService().parse(partial));
        var duplicate = Jsoup.parse(html());
        duplicate.select(".rank").get(1).text("1");
        assertThrows(AppException.class, () -> new MelonChartService().parse(duplicate));
    }
}
```

## src/test/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/MusicOracleTest.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.config.Database;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.dao.Jdbc;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service.*;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

import java.nio.file.*;
import java.sql.*;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.concurrent.*;

@Tag("oracle")
class MusicOracleTest {
    @TempDir Path folder;

    @Test
    void automaticProfileRealCatalogAndTransactionalDemoPayment() throws Exception {
        LocalProfileService profiles = new LocalProfileService();
        var user = profiles.resolve(folder.resolve("profile.properties"));
        try {
            assertEquals(user.id(), profiles.resolve(folder.resolve("profile.properties")).id());
            MusicLibraryService music = new MusicLibraryService(user);
            MembershipService membership = new MembershipService(user);
            assertEquals(100, music.catalog().size());
            assertEquals(1, music.catalog().get(0).rank());
            assertEquals(100, music.catalog().get(99).rank());
            assertFalse(membership.status().active());
            assertEquals(60, music.authorize(9001).pass().limitSeconds(277));
            assertThrows(AppException.class, () -> music.authorize(101));
            String id = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
            ExecutorService pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2);
            try {
                Callable<Boolean> pay =
                        () -> {
                            membership.purchase(id);
                            return true;
                        };
                for (var result : pool.invokeAll(List.of(pay, pay))) assertTrue(result.get());
            } finally {
                pool.shutdownNow();
            }
            assertEquals(1, membership.history().size());
            assertTrue(membership.status().active());
            assertEquals(277, music.authorize(9001).pass().limitSeconds(277));
            assertEquals(4900, membership.history().get(0).amount());
            membership.cancelLatest();
            assertFalse(membership.status().active());
            assertEquals("CANCELLED", membership.history().get(0).status());
            assertEquals(60, music.authorize(9001).pass().limitSeconds(277));
            membership.purchase(UUID.randomUUID().toString());
            assertTrue(membership.status().active());
            assertEquals(2, membership.history().size());
        } finally {
            try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
                Jdbc.update(c, "DELETE FROM MEMBERSHIP_ORDER WHERE member_id=?", user.id());
                Jdbc.update(c, "DELETE FROM MEMBER WHERE member_id=?", user.id());
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## src/test/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/OracleIntegrationTest.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.config.Database;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.dao.Jdbc;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.*;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.service.*;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.nio.file.*;
import java.sql.*;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.concurrent.*;

/** mvn -Poracle-test test : 실제 Oracle, 기존 행은 변경하지 않고 신규 테스트 행만 정리한다. */
@Tag("oracle")
class OracleIntegrationTest {
    @Test
    void jdbcWorkflowPermissionsRollbackConcurrencyAndPreservation() throws Exception {
        String suffix = Long.toUnsignedString(System.nanoTime(), 36),
                a = "qa_a_" + suffix,
                b = "qa_b_" + suffix;
        long originalSongs, originalArtists;
        List<String> before;
        try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
            originalSongs = Jdbc.number(c, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM SONG");
            originalArtists = Jdbc.number(c, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ARTIST");
            before = baseline(c);
        }
        AuthService auth = new AuthService();
        List<Long> created = new ArrayList<>();
        try {
            auth.register(
                    a,
                    "qa-password".toCharArray(),
                    "qa-password".toCharArray(),
                    "검증 회원 A",
                    "qa@example.com");
            auth.register(
                    b,
                    "qa-password".toCharArray(),
                    "qa-password".toCharArray(),
                    "검증 회원 B",
                    "qa@example.com");
            assertFalse(auth.available(a));
            assertThrows(
                    AppException.class,
                    () ->
                            auth.register(
                                    a,
                                    "a".toCharArray(),
                                    "a".toCharArray(),
                                    "중복",
                                    "qa@example.com"));
            assertThrows(AppException.class, () -> auth.login(a, "wrong".toCharArray()));
            assertThrows(
                    AppException.class,
                    () -> auth.login("user01", "DEMO_HASH_NOT_FOR_AUTH_01".toCharArray()));
            PlaylistService owner = new PlaylistService(auth.login(a, "qa-password".toCharArray()));
            PlaylistService other = new PlaylistService(auth.login(b, "qa-password".toCharArray()));
            long id = owner.create("JDBC 검증", "기존 데이터 보존", false);
            created.add(id);
            assertFalse(owner.detail(id).playlist().isPublic());
            assertTrue(other.mine().isEmpty());
            assertThrows(AppException.class, () -> other.detail(id));
            assertThrows(AppException.class, () -> other.edit(id, "침입", "", true));
            assertThrows(AppException.class, () -> other.add(id, 101));
            assertThrows(AppException.class, () -> other.remove(id, 101));
            assertThrows(AppException.class, () -> other.move(id, 101, 1));
            assertThrows(AppException.class, () -> other.delete(id));
            assertThrows(AppException.class, () -> other.like(id, true));
            owner.add(id, 101);
            owner.add(id, 102);
            owner.add(id, 103);
            assertThrows(AppException.class, () -> owner.add(id, 101));
            assertEquals(List.of(101L, 102L, 103L), ids(owner.detail(id)));
            owner.move(id, 103, -1);
            assertEquals(List.of(101L, 103L, 102L), ids(owner.detail(id)));
            owner.move(id, 101, 1);
            assertEquals(List.of(103L, 101L, 102L), ids(owner.detail(id)));
            // 중간 순서 UPDATE 오류가 나도 전체 트랜잭션을 롤백하는지 검증.
            try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
                c.setAutoCommit(false);
                Jdbc.list(
                        c,
                        "SELECT playlist_id FROM PLAYLIST WHERE playlist_id=? FOR UPDATE",
                        r -> r.getLong(1),
                        id);
                Jdbc.update(
                        c,
                        "UPDATE PLAYLIST_SONG SET play_order=999999 WHERE playlist_id=? AND"
                                + " song_id=103",
                        id);
                assertThrows(
                        SQLException.class,
                        () ->
                                Jdbc.update(
                                        c,
                                        "UPDATE PLAYLIST_SONG SET play_order=999999 WHERE"
                                                + " playlist_id=? AND song_id=101",
                                        id));
                c.rollback();
            }
            assertEquals(List.of(103L, 101L, 102L), ids(owner.detail(id)));
            owner.remove(id, 101);
            assertEquals(
                    List.of(1, 2), owner.detail(id).songs().stream().map(Song::order).toList());
            assertTrue(
                    owner.search("네온").stream()
                            .filter(s -> s.id() == 101)
                            .findFirst()
                            .orElseThrow()
                            .artists()
                            .contains("루나"));
            owner.edit(id, "공개 JDBC 검증", "공개 후 좋아요", true);
            assertEquals(2, other.detail(id).songs().size());
            owner.like(id, true);
            other.like(id, true);
            other.like(id, true);
            assertEquals(2, owner.detail(id).playlist().likes());
            assertTrue(other.detail(id).playlist().liked());
            other.like(id, false);
            assertEquals(1, owner.detail(id).playlist().likes());
            // 같은 목록에 대한 동시 추가: 한 연결만 성공하고 나머지는 중복 안내.
            ExecutorService pool = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2);
            try {
                Callable<Boolean> add =
                        () -> {
                            try {
                                owner.add(id, 104);
                                return true;
                            } catch (AppException e) {
                                return false;
                            }
                        };
                var results = pool.invokeAll(List.of(add, add));
                assertNotEquals(results.get(0).get(), results.get(1).get());
                Callable<Boolean> move =
                        () -> {
                            owner.move(id, 104, -1);
                            return true;
                        };
                for (var result : pool.invokeAll(List.of(move, move))) assertTrue(result.get());
            } finally {
                pool.shutdownNow();
            }
            assertEquals(
                    List.of(1, 2, 3), owner.detail(id).songs().stream().map(Song::order).toList());
            owner.edit(id, "다시 비공개", "", false);
            assertThrows(AppException.class, () -> other.detail(id));
            assertThrows(AppException.class, () -> other.like(id, true));
            owner.like(id, false);
            owner.delete(id);
            try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
                assertEquals(
                        0,
                        Jdbc.number(
                                c, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM PLAYLIST_SONG WHERE playlist_id=?", id));
                assertEquals(
                        0,
                        Jdbc.number(
                                c, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM PLAYLIST_LIKE WHERE playlist_id=?", id));
                assertEquals(originalSongs, Jdbc.number(c, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM SONG"));
                assertEquals(originalArtists, Jdbc.number(c, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ARTIST"));
            }
        } finally {
            try (Connection c = Database.open()) {
                for (long id : created)
                    Jdbc.update(
                            c, "DELETE FROM PLAYLIST WHERE playlist_id=? AND member_id=?", id, a);
                Jdbc.update(c, "DELETE FROM MEMBER WHERE member_id IN (?,?)", a, b);
                assertEquals(before, baseline(c), "기존 예시 데이터가 보존되어야 합니다.");
            }
        }
    }

    private static List<Long> ids(PlaylistDetail d) {
        return d.songs().stream().map(Song::id).toList();
    }

    private static List<String> baseline(Connection c) throws SQLException {
        List<String> rows = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String table :
                List.of(
                        "MEMBER",
                        "ARTIST",
                        "SONG",
                        "PLAYLIST",
                        "SONG_ARTIST",
                        "PLAYLIST_SONG",
                        "PLAYLIST_LIKE"))
            rows.addAll(
                    Jdbc.list(
                            c,
                            "SELECT * FROM " + table,
                            r -> {
                                StringBuilder s = new StringBuilder(table);
                                for (int i = 1; i <= r.getMetaData().getColumnCount(); i++)
                                    s.append('|').append(r.getString(i));
                                return s.toString();
                            }));
        Collections.sort(rows);
        return rows;
    }
}
```

## src/test/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/PlaybackTest.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.audio.*;
import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.*;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.io.*;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

import javax.sound.sampled.*;

class PlaybackTest {
    @Test
    void freePreviewCannotSeekBeyondSixtySeconds() {
        PlaybackPolicy p = new PlaybackPolicy(PassStatus.free());
        assertEquals(60, p.limit(276));
        assertEquals(60, p.clampSeek(120, 276));
        assertEquals(0, p.clampSeek(-10, 276));
        assertEquals(45, p.limit(45));
    }

    @Test
    void activePassUnlocksFullSongAndCancellationRestoresLimit() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        PlaybackPolicy p = new PlaybackPolicy(new PassStatus(now.plusDays(30), now));
        assertEquals(276, p.limit(276));
        assertEquals(120, p.clampSeek(120, 276));
        p.update(PassStatus.free());
        assertEquals(60, p.limit(276));
    }

    @Test
    void expiredPassDoesNotUnlockPlayback() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        PlaybackPolicy p = new PlaybackPolicy(new PassStatus(now.minusSeconds(1), now));
        assertEquals(60, p.limit(276));
    }

    @Test
    void allBundledRecordingsAreDecodableRealMp3Files() throws Exception {
        for (String name :
                new String[] {
                    "nocturne.mp3", "fantasie.mp3", "minute-waltz.mp3", "grande-valse.mp3"
                }) {
            try (InputStream file = getClass().getResourceAsStream("/audio/" + name)) {
                assertNotNull(file, name);
                try (AudioInputStream compressed =
                        AudioSystem.getAudioInputStream(new BufferedInputStream(file))) {
                    AudioFormat f = compressed.getFormat();
                    AudioFormat pcm =
                            new AudioFormat(
                                    AudioFormat.Encoding.PCM_SIGNED,
                                    f.getSampleRate(),
                                    16,
                                    f.getChannels(),
                                    f.getChannels() * 2,
                                    f.getSampleRate(),
                                    false);
                    try (AudioInputStream decoded =
                            AudioSystem.getAudioInputStream(pcm, compressed)) {
                        byte[] bytes =
                                decoded.readNBytes(
                                        (int) (pcm.getFrameRate() * pcm.getFrameSize() * 5));
                        assertTrue(bytes.length > 8192);
                        boolean nonzero = false;
                        for (byte b : bytes) nonzero |= b != 0;
                        assertTrue(nonzero, name);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## src/test/java/kr/ac/yumin/playlist/ui/DesignCapture.java

```java
package kr.ac.yumin.playlist.ui;

import kr.ac.yumin.playlist.model.*;

import java.awt.*;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.lang.reflect.*;
import java.nio.file.*;
import java.util.List;

import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import javax.swing.*;

/** 디자인 QA 전용 픽스처. 운영 앱은 이 데이터를 사용하지 않으며 저장 동작은 실행하지 않는다. */
public final class DesignCapture {
    private static void field(Object o, String key, Object value) throws Exception {
        Field f = o.getClass().getDeclaredField(key);
        f.setAccessible(true);
        f.set(o, value);
    }

    private static void call(Object o, String method) throws Exception {
        Method m = o.getClass().getDeclaredMethod(method);
        m.setAccessible(true);
        m.invoke(o);
    }

    private static void save(MainFrame frame, String name) throws Exception {
        JComponent content = (JComponent) frame.getContentPane();
        BufferedImage image =
                new BufferedImage(
                        content.getWidth(), content.getHeight(), BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_RGB);
        Graphics2D g = image.createGraphics();
        content.printAll(g);
        g.dispose();
        ImageIO.write(image, "png", Path.of("docs/screenshots/" + name + ".png").toFile());
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
        Files.createDirectories(Path.of("docs/screenshots"));
        MainFrame[] holder = new MainFrame[1];
        SwingUtilities.invokeAndWait(
                () -> {
                    Ui.theme();
                    holder[0] = new MainFrame();
                    holder[0].setVisible(true);
                });
        Thread.sleep(700);
        SwingUtilities.invokeAndWait(
                () -> {
                    try {
                        MainFrame f = holder[0];
                        save(f, "10_home_disconnected");
                        if (args.length > 0 && args[0].equals("preview")) {
                            var names =
                                    new String[] {
                                        "녹턴 2번, Op. 9 No. 2",
                                        "환상 즉흥곡, Op. 66",
                                        "강아지 왈츠, Op. 64 No. 1",
                                        "화려한 대왈츠, Op. 18"
                                    };
                            var albums =
                                    new String[] {
                                        "Nocturnes, Op. 9",
                                        "Fantaisie-Impromptu, Op. 66",
                                        "Waltzes, Op. 64",
                                        "Grande valse brillante, Op. 18"
                                    };
                            var files =
                                    new String[] {
                                        "nocturne.mp3",
                                        "fantasie.mp3",
                                        "minute-waltz.mp3",
                                        "grande-valse.mp3"
                                    };
                            int[] duration = {277, 326, 108, 320};
                            var rows = new java.util.ArrayList<MediaTrack>();
                            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
                                rows.add(
                                        new MediaTrack(
                                                new Song(
                                                        9001 + i,
                                                        names[i],
                                                        "Frédéric Chopin",
                                                        albums[i],
                                                        "클래식",
                                                        duration[i],
                                                        0),
                                                "audio/" + files[i],
                                                "Musopen",
                                                "https://archive.org/details/musopen-chopin",
                                                "CC0 1.0"));
                            field(f, "tracks", List.copyOf(rows));
                            Field label = MainFrame.class.getDeclaredField("connection");
                            label.setAccessible(true);
                            ((JLabel) label.get(f))
                                    .setText("디자인 확인용 화면 · 실제 저장·재생은 Oracle 연결 후 사용");
                            call(f, "render");
                            f.validate();
                            save(f, "11_home_design_preview");
                            field(f, "page", "전체 곡");
                            call(f, "render");
                            f.validate();
                            save(f, "12_library_design_preview");
                        }
                        f.dispose();
                    } catch (Exception e) {
                        throw new RuntimeException(e);
                    }
                });
        System.exit(0);
    }
}
```
